# Player identity and franchise development overhaul

## Active work — S120 (S119 release note and carried truth)

Selected audit: `docs/AUDIT_2026-10-23.json`. Every premise was read on the live tree at c6e9d691 (5/5 verified).

1. **S119 release note.** Add a dated 2026-10-23 note to `public/status.html`: the phone Desk fits the screen, and passer ratings now match the box score, so strong seasons in existing saves read about ten points higher. Before committing, check that no `tests-ui` spec or helper and no `responsive-evidence` check depends on the copy. Acceptance: the built `status-archive.html` and `changelog.xml` carry the note, and the public tests pass.
2. **Capture channel parity.** `capture-public-qa.mjs` honors `PLAYWRIGHT_CHANNEL` the same way `responsive-evidence.mjs` does. Acceptance: a parity test that goes red on the bare launch.
3. **Weekly standings from the sport pack.** `weeklySimulator` builds conference tables from `structure.conferences`. Acceptance: football output is unchanged, a pack with no conferences emits only `overall`, and the test goes red on the literal version.
4. **Stale board item.** Close the Phase 1 passer-rating item with a pointer to the S119 guard.

Deferred, with evidence: conference display names (a naming step with IP weight and test-id dependence) and payload compaction (the S119 measurement stands).

Then: the canonical suite, staging verification of the exact candidate, the authorized production promotion and readback, and the canonical S120 closeout.

## Previous work — S119 (mobile Desk layout and stat truth)

Selected audit: `docs/AUDIT_2026-10-22.json`. Every premise was measured on the live tree (6/6 verified).

1. **Phone Desk column blowout.** `.team-mode-switch { width: max-content }` contributes its single-row width to grid intrinsic sizing, where `max-width: 100%` cannot resolve. The situational-calls switch therefore sets a 356 px minimum on the 321 px Desk column, and every card ends at x 391 on a 390 px phone. Fix it at the switch with a shrink-wrapping width and wrapping. Acceptance: a browser regression at 390 and 320 px that goes red when the old rule is restored.
2. **One passer rating.** The stat schema and the client clamp the yards term before scaling it; the box score scales first, which is correct. Fix both and hold all three to published reference values (99.3, 158.3, 0, 39.6). Make the golden-master change deliberately and attribute it in a new versioned fixture; the prior fixtures stay byte-identical.
3. **Stale payload premise.** Close the carried 16.2 MB task with the measured 15.305 MB. Compaction is deferred with its measurement and is not claimed as done.

Then: the canonical suite, staging verification of the exact candidate, the authorized production promotion and readback, and the canonical S119 closeout.

## Complete appearance requirements — explicit delivery contract

### One recognizable person, with measurement-compatible variation

Treat the requested hair, skin, eyes, face and physique variation as required product behavior. Resolve one saved person, then use that same authority in roster, draft, dossier, profile and animated body views. The delivery order is background sampling → natural appearance → current grooming → measured skeleton → constrained regional build → athletic motion. Each stage keeps its own evidence and uncertainty.

| Implementation boundary | Concrete requirement and acceptance |
| --- | --- |
| Background → appearance | Country-conditioned, source-versioned background probabilities with mixed/diaspora cases and within-country variation. Country, reporting category and response order cannot force a skin/hair/eye bundle or one face. Check both compatible league totals and within-country diversity. |
| Natural appearance → grooming | Save hair curvature, thickness, density, hairline and natural scalp/brow/beard pigment separately from haircut, part, length, protective arrangement, treatment and dye. Include black/brown/blond/red natural shades and gray progression; demonstrate that changing style or dye preserves the underlying person. Palette support is required; prevalence remains a declared prior where representative data are absent. |
| Pigment → visible person | Continuous skin shade/undertone and iris shade/pattern, individually varying face, ears, nose, jaw, chin, lips and brows. The same saved pigments must agree across face, neck, hands and limbs under shared lighting. Inspect recognizable differences at normal game size, including dark/light themes and mobile. |
| Measurements → skeleton | Honor stature, mass, reach, arm/leg segments, hand/foot dimensions and compatible frame/girth observations. Keep observed and estimated dimensions separate, with units, anatomical endpoints, date, method and uncertainty. Hair, shoes and pads cannot redefine measured height or body breadth. |
| Skeleton + mass → build | Fit shoulder/rib/pelvic frame and regional chest/back/abdomen/arm/thigh/calf breadth and depth jointly. Reconcile complete represented mass and disclose exclusions/conflicts. Equal height/weight must permit different compatible frames and builds; adding weight cannot merely enlarge the whole character or lengthen bones. |
| Anatomy + capacities → athletic expression | Preserve measured bones while speed, acceleration, jump, strength, cutting and endurance drive separate motion controls. Use compatible observed body/performance relationships where available and variable, declared estimates elsewhere. Do not infer exact muscle/fat from a rating. Require heavy-fast, light-slow, equal-build/different-capacity and equal-capacity/different-build comparisons. |
| Career → continuity | Trades, clothing, camera, haircut and save/load preserve identity. Dated weight/build, graying and thinning changes update that person without rerolling their face or natural traits. Validate actual career flows and both browser runtimes. |

The [research ledger's instrument compatibility section](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#source-instrument-compatibility-and-save-continuity) adds the U.S. Census's updated combined-question/Middle Eastern or North African classification alongside historical source instruments. Retain raw responses and instrument versions; future demographic mappings must preserve the saved appearance. This update specifies required behavior and checks. It does not claim complete renderer quality, current population calibration or final career verification.

The player generator and every portrait/body view must produce visibly distinct people across the following channels. These requirements apply to the whole overhaul; a passing hair component does not complete them.

| Channel | Saved authority and implementation | Required visible comparison |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Hair | Natural texture/thickness/pigment plus separate cut, arrangement, length, density, part, treatment, dye and dated thinning/graying | Bald/shaved, short/long, straight/wavy/curly/coily, supported protective styles; front/profile attachment and believable roots/tips at game size |
| Skin and eyes | Continuous pigment/undertone and saved iris variation; one lighting/material pipeline for all exposed regions | Face/ears/neck/hands/limbs agree; dark/light skin and iris variants remain readable in both themes without altering facial anatomy |
| Face and facial hair | Individually varying cranial/face proportions, brows, beard/moustache coverage and independent grooming colors | Recognizable distinct faces, coherent anatomy, scalp/brow/beard differences and age changes; no mandatory face template per race |
| Frame and proportions | Observed height, weight, reach, segment/hand/foot measures and girths where available; explicitly estimated missing dimensions with uncertainty | Equal height/weight with different frames; same frame with different weight; common-scale front/profile silhouettes, valid contacts and measured endpoints |
| Regional build | Dated regional muscle/fat estimates constrained by available observations and full modeled mass accounting | Plausible shoulders/trunk/pelvis/limbs without a universal muscularity slider; disclose fit conflicts and unmodeled mass |
| Athletic movement | Separate speed, acceleration, jump, strength, agility and endurance capacities through the saved skeleton | Identical anatomy with different capacities, heavy-fast/light-slow cases, timed motion and coherent joints/contact |
| Background | Separate birthplace, upbringing, family origins and self-identity, with mixed/diaspora/unknown cases and source-versioned sampling | Compatible country and NFL cohorts checked across independent league seeds; natural team variation rather than fixed quotas |

Country and NFL demographic observations inform probabilistic fictional backgrounds. They do not supply measured frequencies for every hair/eye/skin trait, a universal world race taxonomy, or deterministic anatomical templates. Keep supported population observations separate from declared visual priors and deliberately balanced QA fixtures. Background never changes ratings, potential, development, personality or injury formulas.

The October 8 research recheck confirms [multiple contributors to eye color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/) and [hair texture/thickness](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/hairtexture/). The [NFL's 2025 international-born accounting](https://edge-operations.nfl.com/programs-initiatives/international-growth/international-player-pathway) reports 126 rostered during the season/playoffs, including 61 on 53-man active rosters, across 38 countries/territories. Preserve those distinct populations; neither number alone establishes an opening-roster probability. Existing compatible-census/TIDES evidence and its dates remain in the linked research ledger. No new numerical demographic weights are inferred from this recheck.

Complete-person acceptance requires ordinary-size roster/draft/dossier images and common-scale body comparisons across desktop/mobile, themes, equipment and motion, plus saved identity through grooming, aging, trades and named save/load. Distinct seeds, pixel hashes or channel presence alone do not establish attractive or realistic people. Current smooth crowns, simplified faces and straight-sided torsos remain unfinished craft.

## Current person rendering and next delivery priority — October 8

[Frozen material evidence](visual-qa/PLAYER_PERSON_MATERIAL_2026-10-08.json) and [independent review](PLAYER_PERSON_MATERIAL_REVIEW_R1_2026-10-08.json) verify a modest modern material foundation: shared head/body key/fill response, skin/cloth separation, layered iris/sclera and lip/contact detail. Signed shader squares use multiplication and the Fresnel base is bounded. Saved albedo/person/measurements/sampling and the selected head/body meshes remain unchanged. The original legacy/SVG lighting function remains available; colored body views adopt the new material model.

Twenty focused domain/nine browser checks pass on the final build, including105 selected GPU swatches,126 historical legacy head images,42 modern blink restorations and both built named-save clients. Modern head materials intentionally change; their old pixels are not presented as exact. Independent numerical probes find finite bounded output and supported pigment ordering, with localized wet highlights capable of saturation before output clamping. These are selected engineering/art checks, not biological reflectance or full appearance proof.

[The subsequent facial sculpt](visual-qa/PLAYER_FACE_SCULPT_2026-10-08.json) and [independent scoped review](PLAYER_FACE_SCULPT_REVIEW_R1_2026-10-08.json) verify modestly reduced cheek/muzzle swelling and a narrower upper eye opening. Scalp and pole collars, saved identity and legacy decoding remain covered.39 unique focused domain checks and9 unique browser cases eventually pass; failed mixed runs and the unknown-cause initial worker opening timeout remain recorded, rather than being described as wholly green runs.18 closed-globe-removal comparisons and101 source-bound captures support this limited refinement.

The inspected faces remain plastic/planar, ears/beards/neck simplified and torso silhouettes slab-like. Further delivery must improve coherent adult facial anatomy and anatomical torso/limb/garment contours through the same saved person. Preserve recorded measurements, recognizable identity, scalp/ear/eye attachment, closed blinks, legacy decoding and all views/motion. Refit and verify mass constraints where geometry changes. Inspect ordinary-size before/after images; do not substitute another minor material or envelope receipt for attractive complete people. Population calibration, whole-project tests, the fresh30-season managed career and release remain open.

## Expanded demographic and complete appearance delivery — October 8

The latest hairstyle/pigment/frame clarification is mandatory scope. The [expanded primary-source country evidence and acceptance specification](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#expanded-country-evidence-and-delivery-specification--october-8) adds U.S., England/Wales, Canadian, Brazilian and New Zealand census instruments, with explicit overlapping-response and geographic-scope rules. Preserve mixed backgrounds and diaspora; country of birth supplies probabilistic context. General resident shares and dated NFL player shares require separate recruitment/calibration models.

Delivery sequence:

1. Extend the source ledger and generator provenance in `src/data/playerDemographicBaseline.js` and `src/domain/playerDemographicModel.js`; distinguish native identity classifications, family origins, birthplace and upbringing. Validate compatible dated NFL aggregate margins and disclose unsupported country-conditioned recruitment priors. Keep demographic identity outside gameplay ability/development/injury formulas.
2. Complete the versioned natural-person/grooming contract in `src/domain/playerIdentity.js` and `src/domain/playerVisualIdentity.js`: all hair arrangements/textures/colors, skin pigment/undertone, eyes, face, brows/beard, dye, graying/thinning and aging. Natural pigment remains saved separately from dated styling. Use supported trait correlations with individual variation, not categorical face/palette templates.
3. Complete `src/domain/playerMeasurements.js` and the shared renderer's measured skeleton, frame, regional build and full mass accounting. Preserve observed stature/reach/segment dimensions and uncertainty for estimates. Produce equal-height/weight different-frame examples and fixed-frame different-weight examples. Athleticism controls independently measured movement capacities; it cannot uniquely determine body composition.
4. Deliver a rendered coverage gallery plus actual dossier checks: every visible channel changed with other person fields held fixed; coherent exposed skin; hair attachment and profile silhouettes; eye readability; compatible front/profile body proportions and six movement modes; heavy-fast/light-slow examples; equipment on/off, every theme, desktop/mobile. Verify saved identity through trade, aging and named save/load. Review population frequencies separately from deliberately balanced visual fixtures.

The baseline controlled long-hair comparison had different pixel signatures but nearly identical hanging silhouettes. The current refinement below makes envelopes visibly different; complete curl/lock naturalism remains **unaccepted texture coverage**. Refine the visible curl pattern, lock arrangement and volume together, preserving scalp attachment, face identity and saved natural texture. Reinspect front/profile at normal portrait size; a texture label or pixel difference cannot close this requirement. The current gallery is a diagnostic fixture, not representative demographic sampling or completed appearance proof.

For body proportions, fit stature, reach and segment endpoints first, then frame breadth/depth and regional tissue distribution against mass and any observed girths. Preserve recorded measurements when estimates conflict and report residuals rather than silently changing the athlete. Render equal-height/equal-weight people with different compatible frames, fixed-frame people with different weights, and heavy-fast/light-slow people. Athletic capacities govern movement and exertion through that same measured skeleton; visual muscularity remains an uncertain body-state estimate rather than a direct conversion from a rating.

Completion requires both visible distinctive people and measurement/population evidence. Existing scoped geometry and integration receipts do not satisfy this entire checklist. This update changes the researched implementation requirements; it adopts no invented NFL hair/eye frequencies or new runtime demographic weights.

## Complete person contract — latest appearance clarification

The additional [current NFL cohort check](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#additional-current-cohort-check--october-8) reinforces separate birthplace, upbringing, family origin and heritage fields. Heritage participation does not supply birthplace/race frequencies. Keep cohort dates and denominators alongside each observed margin before adopting sampling weights.

The requested result is visibly different people: hairstyle, natural/dyed hair color, skin pigment and undertone, eye color, facial anatomy, skeletal frame, proportions and athletic movement. This is a required deliverable, not optional polish. Use the [research and acceptance matrix](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#latest-evidence-recheck-and-single-channel-acceptance).

Implement through the existing demographic, identity, measurement and rendering modules. Keep one versioned saved person authority for every view. Resolve country/upbringing/family context probabilistically with mixed backgrounds and diaspora; reconcile compatible historical NFL player margins separately from resident country statistics. Persist natural traits, current grooming, skeletal dimensions, dated regional build and athletic capacities as distinct channels with supported correlations and individual variation. Imported measurements override estimates; record uncertainty for dimensions absent from height/weight data.

Acceptance requires matched changes to each channel at ordinary game size: hair style without pigment/face changes; dye without natural-pigment changes; iris differences without facial changes; frame/proportion differences at equal height and weight; different mass at fixed stature; and different acceleration/speed/jump/strength/endurance through identical anatomy. Include heavy-fast and light-slow players. Verify standing height, skeleton endpoints, girths where available, mass residuals, contacts and silhouette across front/profile/motion, equipment, themes and mobile. Race/background changes cannot alter football ratings, potential, development, personality or injury formulas. Recheck continuity through saves, trades, aging and seasons.

The latest source retrieval reconfirms the existing 2023 TIDES player counts; it does not establish current 2026 race margins. Correct the source locator to Table 1, printed page 45, physical PDF page 47 (zero-based index 46). The existing 4% international-born share and country weights remain declared assumptions pending a compatible dated cohort. The NFL heritage program and birthplace list must not be pooled. No new runtime demographic or anatomical coefficients are adopted by this plan update. Complete visual and population acceptance remains open.

## Current character volume proxy — October 8

Current-body accounting now uses the actual production body and actual full-head skin shell through the physical shader's crown-subtracted coordinate transform. External soles and all head detail/hair surfaces are explicitly excluded; fragment apertures are not solid booleans. [Frozen numerical evidence](CURRENT_CHARACTER_VOLUME_FIT_2026-10-08.json) and [independent review](CURRENT_CHARACTER_VOLUME_FIT_REVIEW_R1_2026-10-08.json) verify this limited research adapter, not complete rendered anatomy or biological mass. Eight focused tests pass. Initial coordinate and overly strict cache-comparison mistakes are retained as superseded evidence; final crown/chin bounds and shader comparisons resolve them.

Four 80-inch same-person masses180/240/350/500lb have unadjusted proxy-volume deficits about5.0–12.7%. Three-step neutral fits at grid64 scale estimated torso/limb breadth/depth about1.029–1.081 while preserving the saved person, skeleton and head. Two-axis grid128 rechecks have maximum residual0.342%, zero unbalanced/negative rays in these selected cases. Axis/refinement agreement is not a true-error or self-intersection certificate.

Do not adopt this iterative quadrature in the live dossier yet. It changes estimated sections, requires measured-girth constraints and complete head/detail accounting, and has substantial local fitting cost. A fast constrained body-state model, pose-volume/deformation checks and actual rendered acceptance must precede runtime adoption. Existing old clay fits are historical. The ongoing117-file core run began before the new adapter test was registered; its result must be reported separately from the eight focused checks and cannot establish the final whole-project outcome.

## Limb CPU update — October 8

The limb writer replaces per-vertex radial/tangent/normal arrays with scalar arithmetic, retaining the original operation order and Float32 staging. Frame, section, continuous-point, cap and index helpers remain unchanged. [Frozen evidence](visual-qa/PLAYER_LIMB_CPU_2026-10-08.json) binds 13 domain/six browser checks, 294 selected poses / 1,176 byte-exact limb comparisons, 24 full pixel comparisons, 52 captures and both built save/load clients. [Independent review](PLAYER_LIMB_CPU_REVIEW_R1_2026-10-08.json) also checks 144 configurations across four buffer types, offsets and degenerate behavior, with 2.37 million exact values. The saved measured surface, head, palette, bend repair and gameplay randomness are unchanged.

Three alternating local synchronized draw trials have before medians 19/19/12.5 ms and after 4.4/7.9/3.2 ms. Geometry-update medians improve from 13.6 to 2.1 ms; update p95 improves from 26.3 to 17.2 ms. This establishes a local improvement, without promising physical-device performance or a universal frame rate. Variation remains, and full anatomical craft, mass/deformation, population calibration, final feature/30-season verification and release stay open. Earlier batching timings below describe the previous slower writer.

## Complete visible variation — source and delivery refinement, October 8

The latest direction requires visible differences in hairstyle, hair color, skin color, eye color, face, frame and proportions. A national resident distribution is contextual evidence, not a conditional distribution of NFL recruits born there.

Delivery must prove three distinct outcomes: coherent individual variation; compatible population sampling with observed/assumed provenance; and measurement-compatible anatomy. Require visibly distinguishable cuts, curl geometry, length, density, hairline, natural/dyed scalp and facial hair, skin pigment/undertone, iris pigment/pattern and facial anatomy. Keep natural traits separate from dated grooming, graying and body state. No identity category selects one compulsory face or palette. Preserve mixed families and locally born diaspora. Appearance/background changes must leave ratings, development, personality, injury behavior and simulation randomness unchanged.

The earlier unintegrated `public/lib/playerBody3d.js` experiment, now located in `test-support/appearance/playerBody3d.js`, builds one inch-based clay mesh for torso, limbs, shared skull skin, hands and feet using the existing measured pose authority. Its focused checks cover neutral plus six movement modes at three phases, four stature/mass tails, skeletal roots/segment lengths and fingertip/foot endpoints. The earlier gallery renders four masses of one80-inch person through identical physical cameras. The recorded prototype is diagnostic evidence only: no production dossier integration, complete anatomical volume union or finished naturalism is claimed. The contextual SVGs use a different camera and cannot establish matched physical scale against the clay images.

Required next work: close and audit solids; reconcile intersections and complete union volume; fit regional build with explicit residuals while retaining measured endpoints; smooth anatomical joins and contours; integrate the saved head, pigment and grooming with coherent body materials. Inspect ordinary-size neutral/profile/three-quarter and all movements in the actual game, desktop/mobile and every theme. Current clay shapes, abrupt joins and heavy-player mass distribution remain quality defects. Equal-height/different-mass, equal-height/mass/different-frame and fixed-anatomy/different-capacity comparisons are mandatory; athleticism must express movement without prescribing a muscularity template.

The historical [assembled-component topology diagnostic](PLAYER_BODY_SOLID_TOPOLOGY_DIAGNOSTIC_2026-10-08.json) groups tube sides with their actual caps before welding coincident seams at a declared1e-5-inch grid tolerance. ## Complete body foundation — next required implementation

The [current reserved-volume diagnostic](PLAYER_RESERVED_VOLUME_DIAGNOSTIC_2026-10-08.json) integrates the nominal skull-skin mesh at the body's `fitSkull` scale. Nine generated positional examples have a maximum last-refinement difference of 0.5332%; the oriented-area closure residual is below 1.8e-9. A controlled 80-inch player retains the same saved face, modeled skull volume and hand/foot lengths at four different masses, while the fixed reserve changes with mass. This quantifies a missing accounting contract; it does not establish a biological head mass, complete union or an incorrect measured body-fat percentage. [Independent diagnostic PASS](PLAYER_RESERVED_VOLUME_DIAGNOSTIC_REVIEW_R1_2026-10-08.json) reproduces all27 mesh integrations and four mass pairs, validates a known24-unit solid under translation/reversed winding, and traces the actual net head scale. The area residual does not prove watertightness or absence of self-intersections; refinement difference is not a certified true-error bound. This does not count shader cutouts, ears, eye solids or the complete body union.

Prioritize a shared complete body surface over further isolated cosmetic head adjustments. A camera-independent body must use the existing measured bone centers, segment lengths and regional breadth/depth profiles, with the shared head in the same physical coordinate system. Define modeled hands and anatomical feet with explicit depth estimates; footwear, clothing, hair and padding remain external geometry. Body views must render that same surface in front/profile/three-quarter and the six existing movement modes, with smooth joins and coherent lighting.

Complete accounting must include closed head/neck/torso/limb/hand/foot solids, root caps and intersections. Report component gross volumes, subtracted overlap, total union volume, target mass under the disclosed artistic density, fit residual and numerical refinement. A sum of allocated percentages is insufficient. Require controlled equal-height/different-mass and equal-height/mass/different-build cases, plus measurement tails and unsupported records. Preserve saved person traits, bone lengths, natural pigment and simulation RNG. Numerical mass agreement still requires direct ordinary-size and common-scale visual inspection before accepting realistic bodies. The current SVG bodies and fixed reserve remain in progress until that replacement is integrated and verified.

## Player appearance delivery priorities — October 8 clarification

The latest request requires complete visible individuality. Finish in this order: calibrated background context; persistent natural appearance and grooming; a measurement-compatible skeleton and regional body; movement through that same body; inspected game-size and career-continuity evidence. The [research supplement](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#seasonal-body-change-and-concrete-delivery-priorities--october-8) adds a directly reviewed 2026 longitudinal primary abstract and makes the remaining calibration work explicit.

| Deliverable | Required acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Country and NFL population model | Dated player cohorts and compatible birthplace/background observations, with mixed families, diaspora, unknown responses and uncertainty. Keep unsupported conditionals labeled assumptions; hairstyle or pigment coverage examples cannot establish population frequency. |
| Individual head and appearance | Recognizably different cuts, texture, length, density, hairlines, scalp/brow/beard colors, skin shades/undertones, iris colors and facial anatomy. Matched examples isolate each channel; complete people must also look coherent and attractive at ordinary game size. |
| Physique | Common-scale unpadded front/profile comparisons match height, mass and observed segment measurements. Include different mass at equal height, different compatible proportions at equal height/mass, and genuine measurement outliers. Declared assumptions cover missing girths/composition; equipment cannot conceal a failed fit. |
| Athletic motion and development | Preserve bone lengths and contact geometry while speed, acceleration, jump, cutting and endurance alter timing and displacement. Include heavy-fast/light-slow players and one body with different capacities. Dated body changes follow explicit observations/events; rating changes alone cannot rewrite anatomy. |
| Persistent person | Haircut, dye, age, training, trade and save/load preserve natural identity. Inspect roster, draft, dossier and animation on desktop/mobile in every theme. Numeric uniqueness and nonzero pixels are insufficient visual proof. |

These are required outcomes, not a claim that the renderer or population fit is complete. Existing scoped evidence and unresolved craft defects remain authoritative.

### Concrete appearance work and completion criteria

Implement the latest direction through the existing saved-person pipeline. The following are required implementation tasks, with current gaps distinguished from existing recipe support:

| Work area and owner files | Required change | Completion criterion |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Background sampling: `src/data/playerDemographicBaseline.js`, `src/domain/playerDemographicModel.js` | Preserve the historical NFL response counts separately from birthplace, upbringing and family origins. Replace illustrative country weights only when compatible evidence supports the replacement; retain unknowns and mixed/diaspora cases. Every probability table records its cohort, denominator, date and measured/assumed status. | Report league and prospect distributions across independent seeds, with uncertainty and unsupported conditional cells. World resident shares cannot stand in for NFL selection. No fixed team quotas. |
| Natural appearance: `src/domain/playerVisualIdentity.js`, `src/domain/playerIdentity.js` | Audit the existing survey-category complexion-band fallback. Its broad numeric bands are rendering priors, not measured pigmentation distributions. A new recipe must sample individual continuous pigments with explicit provenance and within-background variation; retain old saved recipes and decoders. Keep natural scalp, brow and beard colors distinct but coherent, skin shade/undertone, iris pigment/pattern, facial anatomy, hair curvature, thickness, density and hairline. | Matched people visibly differ within the same country/background. Changing a reporting label does not recolor an already saved person. Missing joint trait evidence stays an assumption; no invented genotype or deterministic race-to-face mapping. |
| Hair and grooming: saved recipes and shared head renderers | Render black, brown, blond, red and gray natural shades plus separate dye; brown, hazel/amber, green, blue and gray iris coverage. Render shaved/buzz, fades, cropped/parted hair, waves, curls/coils, afros, braids, locs and longer/tied arrangements where growth length and geometry support them. Include brow, mustache, beard and thinning variation. These lists specify visual coverage, not equal sampling rates. | Silhouette, strand/clump structure and material detail remain convincing at dossier and roster size. Hair has volume and a scalp attachment rather than a solid cap. Cut/dye changes preserve face, natural pigment, body and identity; helmets occlude rather than replace hair. |
| Frame and tissue: `src/domain/playerMeasurements.js` and shared body surfaces | Fit one skeleton to stature, reach, segment lengths and hand/foot observations; jointly fit shoulder/pelvic breadth, trunk depth and regional tissue to mass and compatible composition evidence. Missing dimensions carry estimation provenance. Complete mass accounting must include head, hands, feet, caps and overlaps rather than rely on the current fixed reserve. | Common-scale front/profile bodies agree with saved measurements. Equal stature/mass permits different compatible torso/leg ratios and builds; equal stature with different mass visibly changes bulk. Hair, footwear, pads and camera zoom cannot masquerade as anatomical dimensions. Report residuals and unsupported fits. |
| Athletic expression: `public/lib/playerMotion.js` and the shared skeleton | Express acceleration, running, cutting, jumping, strength demonstrations and endurance through the measured body, with individually varied posture/timing where supported. A drill result is an observation of that drill, not a direct measure of every athletic capacity or body composition. | One body at different capacities changes timing/displacement while retaining bone lengths. Heavy-fast and light-slow cases remain possible. Feet contact the ground coherently; rating gains do not silently increase muscle or reroll proportions. |
| Career and visual proof: domain persistence and built browser checks | Preserve the person across roster/draft/dossier/game views, grooming, trades, saves, seasons and dated physical changes. Keep demographic frequency checks separate from deliberately balanced visual fixtures. | Inspect front/profile/three-quarter heads and whole bodies at ordinary game size and a common physical scale, on desktop/mobile in every theme. Controlled pairs isolate hair style, natural/dyed color, skin, eyes, face, frame, regional build and capacity. Reject unreadable differences, plastic faces, cap-like hair or mannequin anatomy even when hashes and numeric tests pass. |

Primary-source recheck for this clarification: the [TIDES 2023 NFL report](https://www.tidesport.org/_files/ugd/326b62_f8ae68d67f744fdbba5962c84b43f3bf.pdf), Appendix II player table (zero-based PDF page 46), contains the counts already stored in the baseline: 821 Black, 375 White, 167 two-or-more-races, 136 not disclosed, 27 Hawaiian/Pacific Islander, seven Hispanic/Latino, two Asian and one American Indian/Alaska Native. These are historical instrument responses, not a current worldwide ancestry or pigmentation table. The [Census race FAQ](https://www.census.gov/topics/population/race/about/faq.html), revised August 27, 2026, confirms self-identification and multiple responses; preserve instrument-specific categories when reconciling sources.

The directly reviewed [MedlinePlus hair-color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/haircolor/), [hair-texture](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/hairtexture/) and [eye-color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/) pages support separate continuous pigment and hair geometry channels, with many contributing genes and treatment/age effects. They do not supply NFL country-conditioned frequencies. The indexed primary [411-player NFL DXA study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24149760/) supplies positional/regional body-composition context; direct PubMed retrieval exposed no article text in this recheck. No individual surface measurements, new fitted coefficients or representative haircut frequencies were acquired. Appearance and background remain excluded from talent, potential, personality and injury formulas. This clarification updates the plan; it does not certify finished rendering or calibration.

## Required visible individuality — latest player direction

### Body-reference retrieval follow-through — October 8

The primary [411-player NFL study abstract](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24149760/) was subsequently accessible through the search index; direct retrieval still returned HTTP 429. It distinguishes regional lean/fat mass across positions, including similar total lean mass but different fat mass for offensive and defensive linemen. This supports allowing distinct builds at similar mass; it does not provide external shoulder widths, surface meshes, individual covariance or a mapping from athletic ratings to tissue. No numeric coefficients were adopted from the abstract.

The directly retrieved [467-player collegiate study](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5591058/) used four universities, ages 17–24 and selected preseason scans from repeated observations. Keep that cohort separate from NFL veterans and retain its scan/participant distinction. **Implementation acceptance:** fit stature, segment lengths, external breadth/depth and regional bulk jointly; verify equal-height/mass players can have distinct compatible builds. Athleticism changes movement and demonstrated capacity without silently changing anatomy. The current fixed 12.5% reserved volume and artistic density require explicit complete-body accounting before measurement realism can pass.

Acceptance requires the rendered player, not just saved trait metadata, to show the requested differences:

- Hair: distinct cuts, lengths, textures, density and hairlines; natural color, dye, facial hair and age-related changes remain separately persistent.
- Pigment and face: visibly varied skin shades/undertones, eye colors/patterns and facial structures, with the same person recognizable in portrait, profile, full body and animation.
- Frame and proportions: fit recorded height, weight, reach and limb measurements in physical units. Allow different compatible shoulder/hip widths, torso/leg ratios and regional builds at the same height and weight; preserve observed measurements rather than stretching a generic model.
- Athleticism: use measured or separately modeled speed, acceleration, jump, strength and cutting capacities to drive the fitted body's movement. Test heavy-fast and light-slow players, and the same anatomy at different capacities. A rating alone cannot establish muscle size or body-fat percentage.
- Demographics: reconcile dated NFL player cohorts with country/birthplace context; include diaspora, mixed backgrounds and variation within each country. Keep race/ethnicity reporting separate from visible traits and football attributes. Unsupported conditional frequencies remain explicit assumptions.
- Proof: inspect matched examples at common physical scale and ordinary game size, desktop/mobile and every theme; verify identity through save/load, trades and season transitions. Appearance variation passes only when the intended differences are actually visible.

Primary-source rationale and sampling limits remain in the [appearance research contract](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#appearance-channel-and-cohort-contract--october-8). These are required outcomes; complete visual quality and demographic calibration remain unfinished.

## Complete appearance requirement — researched plan update, October 8

Player generation must produce recognizable individuals with varied backgrounds, hairstyles, natural and current hair colors, skin shades and undertones, eye colors and iris patterns, faces, facial hair and body frames. Bodies must agree with recorded height, weight, reach and limb measurements, and their motion must express separately modeled athletic capacities. This requirement is broader than color swaps or position silhouettes. The [expanded research and acceptance contract](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#appearance-channel-and-cohort-contract--october-8) specifies the channels, sampling limits, fitting sequence and review evidence.

Implementation order: reconcile dated NFL and country source universes; generate persistent person/background authority; generate natural appearance and dated grooming independently; fit linked anatomy and regional build to supported observations; render the same person in every view and motion; verify population calibration separately from visual naturalism. Mixed backgrounds and diaspora are required. Unsupported country-conditioned trait frequencies remain declared assumptions until compatible observations exist. No race, ethnicity, skin, hair or eye modifier may feed football ratings, potential, personality or injury risk. This is an expanded implementation requirement, not evidence of finished rendering or newly calibrated runtime coefficients.

## Complete appearance, regional anatomy and diaspora research — October 8 addition

The requested outcome is a visibly distinct, coherent person in every view. Required variation includes hairstyle, length, texture, density and hairline; natural and presented hair/brow/beard color; continuous skin shade and undertone; iris color and detail; facial proportions; skeletal proportions; and regional body breadth/depth. These are individually saved channels with supported correlations and residual variation. Birthplace informs background probabilities while allowing diaspora and mixed families. Race/background never modifies talent or physical capacities.

The [new research and delivery sequence](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#regional-anatomy-and-pacific-diaspora-extension--october-8) adds two primary athlete studies and separate American Samoa/U.S. Pacific diaspora census observations. It also records the TIDES website migration and unsuccessful replacement-site retrieval; 2023 player counts retain their historical label. No new demographic or body coefficients are adopted by this plan update.

Deliver in this order: (1) source-specific cohort/geography records and compatible country/background conditionals, (2) persistent natural identity and dated grooming, (3) measurement-compatible skeleton and complete regional body fit, (4) capacity-specific movement through that body, (5) actual-size/common-scale visual acceptance and save continuity. Require minority and mixed-background coverage within birthplaces, same-height/weight bodies with different compatible proportions, heavy-fast/light-slow examples, and independent visible comparisons of hair, skin and eye channels. Coverage fixtures are not demographic frequency targets. Whole-person naturalism and population calibration remain unfinished.

## Complete-person requirements and research priorities — October 8 clarification

Every generated player must be visibly individual in haircut, length, texture, hairline/density, natural and presented scalp/brow/beard color, skin shade/undertone, iris color/pattern and facial anatomy. Background sampling must allow different identities and looks within the same birthplace, including diaspora and mixed families. Birth country changes contextual probabilities; it does not select a compulsory race or appearance preset. Retain source-defined identities and unknown responses, and keep these fields outside ability, potential, personality and injury calculations.

The [additional primary research and acceptance matrix](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#complete-person-priority-and-additional-athlete-evidence--october-8) adds NFL regional-composition evidence, split-based sprint evidence and demographic denominator checks. Its observations do not yet supply fitted body or country coefficients.

Prioritize the following work within the existing implementation stages:

1. Repair the demonstrated height/weight presentation clamps across frame, skeleton, static body and motion together. Preserve legacy recipe geometry through versioned decoding; valid new observations must survive all views. Unsupported anatomy needs an explicit fitting result, not silently substituted source measurements. Adjust camera bounds without hiding size differences through per-player zoom.
2. Fit regional breadth/depth and linked segments jointly; reconstruct every drawn region before claiming mass agreement. Preserve uncertainty for missing composition and girths. Equal stature/mass can produce different compatible bodies. Strength or speed alone cannot establish observed muscle size or body-fat percentage.
3. Finish all saved appearance channels in front, profile, body and animation, including ordinary roster/draft/dossier sizes and fallback rendering. Require matched visible comparisons for each channel and recognizable variation within one birthplace; metadata differences alone do not pass.
4. Validate athlete distributions against compatible cohorts, and birth/background sampling against compatible person-level counts. Report historical versus current evidence, missing categories, overlapping responses, selection bias and unsupported conditional cells. Grooming frequencies remain declared artistic priors where evidence is absent.
5. Verify one person's natural identity, dated physical state and grooming through save/load, trades, aging and season transitions. Capacity changes must affect movement through the same anatomy; documented weight/composition changes may update the corresponding body regions.

These are expanded research-backed requirements, not completed implementation. Demographic calibration, whole-person visual acceptance and the broader goal remain open.

## Full appearance and measurable body contract — October 8 follow-up

The user's complete-person requirement applies to every generated player: individually varied hairstyles, natural and presented hair colors, skin shades/undertones, eyes, facial features and body proportions, shared across portraits, profiles and movement. The [research follow-up](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#body-fitting-and-athletic-expression-follow-up--october-8) specifies measurement reconciliation, drill-specific animation and dated regional body changes. Country/background distributions inform probabilistic fictional identities with diaspora and mixed-family variation; they cannot choose a compulsory face, pigment bundle, frame or athletic ability.

Implement the body as linked skeletal segments plus independently variable regional soft tissue. Fit available observations jointly, preserve their precision and anatomical endpoints, and expose missing fields as estimates. Validate the mesh in physical units before camera framing. Resolve measured speed, acceleration, jumping and direction changes through that same rig; do not turn overall rating into body shape. Use a common-scale comparison alongside ordinary game views so zoom cannot hide stature or bulk errors.

The next acceptance work must include regional composition changes at constant recorded mass, matching measured split times without inventing unobserved capacities, explicit handling of incompatible measurements, and recognizable appearance variety within a shared birthplace. These extend planned requirements; no new population or body coefficients are adopted by this documentation change.

Compare equal height/mass with different frames, equal capacity with different bodies, and one unchanged body with different capacities. Fit observed dimensions first; record missing composition/proportions as estimates. ### Required appearance catalog and body comparisons

Implement independently saved controls for cut, length, part, fade, hairline, curl/wave, strand thickness, density, scalp color, brow color, beard color and coverage, dye, graying and thinning. Include shaved/bald, buzz, crop, parted, fades, longer loose hair, braids and locs; eligibility depends on hair length and treatment, with variation across backgrounds. Natural texture and current styled texture are separate. Each eligible style needs a convincing silhouette and attachment in front, three-quarter and profile views, including under helmets and after helmet removal.

Skin shade and undertone must remain coherent across face, ears, neck, arms and legs. Save iris color and pattern separately from lighting; include dark/light brown, hazel, green, blue and intermediate variation. Preserve independently variable brows, facial hair, facial landmarks and restrained asymmetry. Avoid requiring every small iris detail to be readable in a roster thumbnail: inspect detailed traits in the dossier, and require the complete person to remain recognizable at roster size.

Fit skeletal frame and regional volume jointly around recorded measurements. Required visible comparisons include equal stature with substantially different mass; equal stature and mass with different compatible shoulder/pelvic breadth, torso/leg shares and reach; and different chest/waist/thigh/calf distributions without altering observed bones. Neither position nor overall rating selects one body template. Allow heavier fast players and lighter slow players, and varied strength/endurance combinations. Actual drill measurements, where available, drive the corresponding movement demonstration; estimated capacity remains labeled and individually variable.

Delivery evidence must include same-country individuals with visibly different identities, mixed/diaspora backgrounds, matched single-trait changes, complete body silhouettes and movement, and a save/reload plus team-change comparison. Record evidence status separately for demographic frequencies, pigment correlations, grooming frequencies and body fitting. A source describing a mechanism cannot establish national prevalence. The [October 8 research extension](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_DEMOGRAPHICS_EXPANSION_2026-10-07.md#october-8-extension-shape-information-and-natural-versus-styled-hair) supports these requirements; coefficients remain pending compatible observations.

Full naturalism, measured regional-girth reconciliation, empirical demographic/body calibration and final career/release acceptance remain open.

## Expanded demographic and complete-athlete calibration — October 7, 2026

The requested outcome is a league of visibly different, persistent individuals: racial and ethnic backgrounds, mixed family origins, skin shades and undertones, eye colors, scalp/brow/beard colors, hairstyles and textures, facial anatomy, and measurement-compatible bodies. The [expanded evidence and delivery contract](PLAYER_COMPLETE_APPEARANCE_CALIBRATION_2026-10-07.json) records this research pass, retrieval limits and four additional acceptance fixtures. It extends the existing authorities below; it does not establish a completed runtime calibration.

**NFL research upgrade:** the original [2023 TIDES report, Table 1](https://www.tidesport.org/_files/ugd/326b62_f8ae68d67f744fdbba5962c84b43f3bf.pdf) is now retrievable again. Its player row contains 821 Black, 375 White, 167 two-or-more-races, 136 not-disclosed, 27 Hawaiian/Pacific Islander, 7 Hispanic/Latino, 2 Asian and 1 American Indian/Alaska Native responses: 1,536 in total. Preserve these original categories and the nondisclosure denominator. They describe a historical reported cohort, not 2026 frequencies or a measured distribution of skin, hair and eye colors. The historical listing still labels 2023 latest; the replacement website failed retrieval in this pass. A newer racial margin remains unverified.

**Country and recruitment calibration:** extend `src/data/playerDemographicBaseline.js` and `src/domain/playerDemographicModel.js` with evidence records for every supported birth country. Each record needs observation year, population/cohort, age coverage, observational unit, original response schema, multiple-response semantics, denominator, conditional direction, source and uncertainty. Use compatible birthplace-within-population tables where available; resident totals alone do not measure backgrounds among people born there. Separate NFL recruitment pathways from national resident proportions. Preserve locally born diaspora, upbringing, citizenship and multiple family origins. Missing compatible evidence uses a named, versioned simulation assumption. Do not infer a real player's race or natural traits from photographs, surnames or birthplace. Diagnose league-wide distributions across independent seeds with sampling uncertainty; do not force identical racial quotas onto teams or each draft class.

**Natural appearance and presentation:** [MedlinePlus eye-color guidance](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/) supports continuous iris variation and complex inheritance; [hair-color guidance](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/haircolor/) describes separate pigment contributions and variable graying. Implementation inference: keep continuous pigment controls beneath named colors, with correlated individual variation and no fixed background palette. Maintain separate natural scalp/brow/beard color, iris color/pattern, skin shade/undertone, curvature, thickness, density and hairline. Cuts, fades, parts, braids, locs, length, facial-hair styles, dye, treatment and gray coverage are dated presentation choices. Eligible style coverage is not evidence of equal prevalence. Research grooming with a dated roster/prospect sampling frame, trait-level unknowns and occlusion; visibly dyed hair cannot establish natural color. Age progression retains individual onset variation and recognizable identity. No appearance or identity channel modifies talent, intelligence, personality or injury risk.

**Measured frame and motion:** [NASA's anthropometry overview](https://www.nasa.gov/directorates/esdmd/hhp/anthropometry-and-biomechanics/) explicitly recognizes different proportions at equal height and weight. Its [biomechanical-model abstract](https://ntrs.nasa.gov/citations/20210022854) separates measured landmarks, segment lengths and joint coordinates; this is methodological support, not NFL fitting coefficients. Extend `src/domain/playerMeasurements.js` around recorded stature, dated mass, reach, shoulder and pelvic skeletal breadth, limb/hand/foot endpoints, torso/leg shares and distinct regional girths/depths. Preserve units, anatomical sites, posture, method, precision, unknowns and conflicting observations. Fit missing dimensions jointly with residual variation. A circumference alone cannot determine front width and profile depth; mass alone cannot determine body fat. A position change preserves the person. Initial body/capacity relationships require compatible athlete data and held-out validation; speed, acceleration, agility, jump, strength and endurance remain individually variable.

**Integration prerequisite found during research, subsequently repaired:** neutral front arms originally attached using external chest width and added lateral elbow/wrist offsets, while cut-mode roots used skeletal shoulder span. The measured-rig component above repairs that mismatch for current v2 bodies, retaining the v1 decoder. All views must continue consuming shared bone centers and exact segment lengths as recorded regional constraints are added. Extra clearance changes pose angles, never recorded bone roots or lengths. The research pass itself did not implement this repair; the separate component receipt records implementation and verification.

| Delivery order | Concrete acceptance |
| --- | --- |
| 1. Compatible demographic calibration | Each fitted conditional is reconstructable from its source cohort and denominator. Mixed and diaspora backgrounds remain possible. Historical, unknown and assumed weights stay distinguishable. |
| 2. Saved natural person and dated grooming | Inspect matched changes for every pigment, texture, density, hairline, cut, beard and facial channel at roster/draft/dossier size, then complete same-country individuals. Correlations allow variation; grooming preserves the natural recipe. |
| 3. Shared measured rig and regional body | Reconstruct shoulder/hip root spacing and actual segment lengths in neutral/profile/all six motion modes. Cover equal height/different mass, equal height/mass/different proportions, long/short reach and conflicting measurements. |
| 4. Independent athletic expression | Heavy-fast and light-slow examples, plus single-capacity counterfactuals, change the corresponding motion while preserving anatomy unless a dated physical observation changes it. Unknown drills remain unknown. |
| 5. Whole-person and career verification | Inspect desktop/mobile and both themes. Actual grooming, weight/composition events, trades, aging and named-save replacement/load/reload preserve the same individual across every view. Component checks alone do not establish whole-person quality. |

Add `historical-nfl-margin-is-not-current`, `pigment-label-is-not-a-fixed-swatch`, `neutral-rig-preserves-measured-segments` and `unobserved-grooming-is-not-natural-color` to the existing trait acceptance contract. No runtime frequencies, biological coefficients or renderer behavior are changed by this planning update.

## Full appearance and regional silhouette — latest user requirements

Every supported hair style, texture, length, density, hairline, scalp/brow/beard color, skin shade/undertone, iris color/pattern and facial control must produce a visible difference. Preserve supported correlations without locking a country or identity category to a fixed palette. Birthplace, upbringing, family origins and self-identification remain separate saved context. The [NHGRI population-descriptor guidance](https://www.genome.gov/about-genomics/policy-issues/population-descriptors-in-genomics) distinguishes these descriptors and their measurement; it does not supply national appearance frequencies. Demographic calibration remains probabilistic and permits diaspora, mixed families and variation within each background.

**Defect identified before the regional component:** the common-scale 74-inch/180-pound versus 74-inch/330-pound comparison showed a strongly tapered, chest-dominant heavy torso. New v2 sections remove that forced taper; complete naturalism and measured regional fitting remain unresolved. Greater total volume and larger limbs alone do not establish convincing anatomy. A heavier player can have different compatible regional distributions; weight alone cannot establish an abdominal contour or body-fat percentage.

The [football anthropometry study](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3392155/) includes 157 Division IA athletes and separate waist, umbilical, hip, chest and limb circumferences alongside skinfolds and dual-energy X-ray absorptiometry (DXA). Retrieved indexed abstract/results are insufficient for transplanting its equations into current NFL generation. The [seven-week football methods comparison](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC8402408/) studied 29 Division III players and found regional changes were not interchangeable between DXA and bioelectrical impedance analysis (BIA). Retain method, instrument and dated uncertainty when comparing physical changes. Neither study provides a complete NFL body-shape distribution or observed gait.

**Regional-body contract: shared sections and measured fitting.** The artistic section/decoder foundation is now implemented. Extend the existing measurement and saved-body authorities with observed constraints and validated fitting. The full package is complete only when the following behavior is verified:

| Implementation | Required behavior and evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Endpoint-aware observations | Preserve chest, minimal-waist, umbilical and hip girths as distinct observations, including units, collection date, site, posture, method and error bounds. Missing/ambiguous sites retain uncertainty. Do not alias every circumference to `waistRatio`. |
| Joint cross-sections | Resolve saved width, depth and longitudinal location for chest, abdomen and pelvic soft tissue alongside limb sections. Separate pelvic skeletal breadth from external hip width. Fit missing dimensions jointly with individual residuals; a circumference cannot uniquely determine width and depth. |
| Solver and renderer integration | Replace the modern two-endpoint taper in `src/domain/playerMeasurements.js` and `public/lib/torsoSurface.js` with the same resolved section contract consumed by static, profile, bust and articulated renderers. Permit compatible straight, tapered and fuller abdominal forms; no hard assumption that every lower section is narrower than the chest. Preserve observed lengths and mass authority, with explicit residuals when constraints conflict. |
| Versioned persistence | Introduce a new decoder/version for the changed section model. Keep existing regional-body recipes and old save rendering stable; new births use the new version. Actual trade, dated mass change and named-save replacement/load/reload must retain natural identity and skeleton. Estimated volume density and missing composition remain disclosed assumptions. |
| Visual acceptance | Inspect complete front/profile/moving bodies at a common scale and at ordinary game size in both themes and desktop/mobile. Compare equal stature/different mass, equal stature/mass/different compatible regional builds, measured girth/depth cases and heavy-fast/light-slow people. Reject mannequin-like anatomy, disconnected joints and hidden trait changes even when numeric volume checks pass. |

Athleticism controls the corresponding movement through this saved frame: speed, acceleration, cutting, jump, strength and endurance retain separate inputs. Improved ratings do not automatically add muscle, change bone lengths or reroll pigmentation. Measured composition and explicitly estimated physical changes update all views together. Extend the [trait acceptance contract](PLAYER_TRAIT_AUTHORITY_2026-10-07.json) with `regional-section-not-global-taper`, `girth-site-is-not-interchangeable` and `composition-method-continuity`. These are planned acceptance requirements, not completion evidence.

## Appearance research follow-through — October 7, 2026

The requested variation is a required whole-person deliverable: hairstyle, texture, length, density, hairline, natural/dyed scalp color, brows and beard; skin shade/undertone; iris color/pattern; facial anatomy; and a body whose stature, reach, segment proportions and regional bulk agree across every view. Keep the existing five-stage implementation contract below as the integration authority. Every advertised trait needs an inspected matched pair at normal game size, followed by complete individuals; metadata uniqueness alone cannot pass.

The [new research record](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_RESEARCH_FOLLOWTHROUGH_2026-10-07.json) adds three adoption checks:

- **Match the sampling unit.** The recent [South African census article](https://www.statssa.gov.za/?p=19982) reports diversity among co-resident household members. It does not measure an individual's mixed ancestry. Require compatible person-level birthplace/family-origin observations before fitting that probability. Preserve local racial/ethnic categories rather than translating every country's responses into a universal racial palette.
- **Fit actual proportions.** The [elite-athlete stature study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33906546/) motivates testing stature-dependent segment ratios rather than enlarging one skeleton uniformly. Its abstract is not an NFL coefficient table. Observed lengths remain authoritative; missing dimensions need a conditional fit and saved uncertainty.
- **Review full methods before using tables.** The previously inaccessible [Mexican professional-player paper](https://doi.org/10.3390/jfmk11010109) is now available through the publisher PDF. It measures chest width and depth separately, alongside sitting height, breadths and girths. Its listed position counts total188 against a reported189; its measurer descriptions conflict. Retain those discrepancies. Estimated composition, historical cohort selection and unvalidated classification prevent direct adoption as NFL physique or performance probabilities.

The [TIDES historical NFL page](https://www.tidesport.org/nfl) still labels2023 as latest and directs readers to a moved site that could not be retrieved in this check. Existing figures remain dated historical calibration; current NFL racial margins are unverified here. Recruitment, resident populations, player self-identification, natural appearance and grooming each retain their own denominator and provenance.

**Delivery sequence:** finish the coherent adult face/body renderer and visible trait coverage using explicitly labeled artistic priors; replace only supported sampling weights after compatible data review; then run population diagnostics and actual career persistence checks. Athleticism drives the corresponding movement through the saved frame. Height/weight constrain compatible anatomy; a rating increase alone does not reroll bones, pigment or body composition. Add `household-is-not-ancestry`, `exploratory-summary-is-not-joint-fit` and `stature-allometry-preserves-observation` to the trait acceptance contract. Full appearance acceptance remains open.

## Joint physique fitting — research follow-through, October 7, 2026

The required body variation needs a joint fitting step before surface rendering. A [reviewed primary multivariate study](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4698842/) provides a method reference for linked body dimensions. Its historical truck-driver cohort cannot establish football physique frequencies. The [research contract](PLAYER_JOINT_BODY_FITTING_REFERENCE_2026-10-07.json) records reviewed methods, a source-total discrepancy and the still-unreviewed ANSUR raw data.

**Implementation inference:** compare a conditional multivariate fit with a compatible nearest-neighbor residual model using held-out athlete observations. Reconcile recorded stature, dated weight and endpoints first; estimate missing segment lengths, skeletal breadths, girths and front/profile depths jointly. Check conditional covariance and reconstruction error across stature, mass and position strata. Save model version, individual residuals, uncertainty and any extrapolation with the person. Source-defined identity, pigment and grooming do not select physique or athletic ratings.

Keep accommodation boundary examples as deterministic visual stress cases. Fit a supported joint interior distribution for player generation; do not make a handful of boundary bodies equally likely templates. A valid measurement outside reference coverage stays authoritative and explicitly flagged. Statistical rarity alone cannot delete a player, clamp a recorded measurement or reject a heavy-fast athlete. Separate incompatible observations from unsupported model coverage.

Add four acceptance fixtures: `joint-shape-reconstruction`, `reference-tail-preserves-player`, `boundary-model-is-not-prevalence` and `source-total-conflict`. These require linked-dimension diagnostics, unchanged recorded outliers through save restoration, explicit sampling denominators, and arithmetic checks that preserve contradictory source cells. Runtime fitting and whole-player visual acceptance remain pending.

## Additional regional-body requirements — October 7, 2026

The requested variation includes every visible channel: hairstyle, natural and dyed hair color, texture, density, hairline, facial hair, skin shade/undertone, eye color/pattern, facial proportions and body frame. Resolve these through one saved person across roster, draft, dossier, profile and movement. Birthplace contributes probabilistic background context with diaspora and mixed families; football recruitment supplies a separate cohort calibration. Neither selects a fixed face, color palette or athletic ability.

**New body-shape research:** a [2009 primary study abstract](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/19387400/) reports 82 Division III players, measured for stature, mass, regional girths and densitometric body fat. Its position comparisons support researching regional build instead of uniformly widening the entire body. This historical college cohort is not a current NFL distribution. Full methods, joint records and conversion validation remain required before adopting coefficients. The [regional-body reference](PLAYER_REGIONAL_BODY_REFERENCE_2026-10-07.json) records retrieval limits and the planned adoption contract.

Extend the measured-frame step with a conditional regional solver. Reconcile stature, mass and observed endpoints first, then use available sitting height, shoulder/pelvis breadths, chest/waist/hip girths and limb girths. Each observation retains date, posture, anatomical endpoint, units, uncertainty and source. Save a joint distribution with residual variation for missing dimensions, rather than drawing each circumference independently. Estimate front width and profile depth together: circumference alone cannot establish either one. Keep skeletal breadth, soft-tissue girth and body composition distinct. Body mass index alone cannot select body fat, muscularity or an abdominal silhouette.

| Added acceptance fixture | Required outcome |
| --- | --- |
| `regional-girth-constraint` | Compatible observed chest, waist, hip and limb girths constrain the resolved surface while preserving stature, reach and mass authority. Unknown dimensions remain estimates. |
| `equal-girth-different-depth` | Two compatible cross-sections can share a circumference while differing in front width and profile depth. Front/side/motion consume the same saved sections. |
| `skeleton-versus-soft-tissue` | A dated bulk change preserves recorded skeletal breadth and bone lengths; changing mass cannot silently reroll the skeleton. |
| `athleticism-without-body-fat-invention` | Capacity-only changes affect movement without inventing fat or lean-mass observations. A heavy, fast athlete remains possible; skin, eye, hair and identity labels never supply performance modifiers. |

Adoption requires reviewed compatible athlete records, measurement-error bounds, within-position residual diagnostics and held-out reconstruction. Historical college, international professional and current NFL cohorts retain separate labels. Until those checks pass, publish body-shape priors and cross-section assumptions as artistic estimates. Inspect matched measurement cases at common scale and ordinary UI size, then prove continuity through grooming, career changes, trades and actual named-save replacement/load/reload. This extends the implementation plan; it does not close full-player visual acceptance.

Obtain full methods and compatible joint records before fitting; keep animation calibration separate from measured workouts. A 40-yard result cannot establish endurance, reactive agility or regional muscle composition.
