National demographics and football recruitment remain separate calibration layers. Athletic capacity can inform a supported initial joint model, while subsequent rating changes preserve the frame unless a dated physical observation changes it. The whole-player overhaul remains open until the visible and persistence checks above pass.

## Latest clarification: complete appearance and physically compatible athletes

**Demographic and appearance calibration follow-through:** the [new research supplement](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_CALIBRATION_SUPPLEMENT_2026-10-07.json) adds three checked references and concrete extraction, generation and acceptance requirements. Canada supplies a useful conditioning check: 633,785 of the source's 1,547,870 Black residents were born in Canada. This is birthplace conditional on the reported group, not that group's share of Canadian-born people ([Statistics Canada](https://www150.statcan.gc.ca/n1/pub/89-657-x/89-657-x2024005-eng.htm)). Preserve this distinction when researching every supported birth country.

**NFL calibration anchors:** retain the previously verified 2023 TIDES counts and nondisclosure category in the [country evidence inventory](PLAYER_COUNTRY_EVIDENCE_2026-10-06.json), with their original 1,536-player denominator and reporting definitions. The original PDF was unavailable during this recheck; these remain dated historical evidence. The [official international-player page](https://edge-operations.nfl.com/programs-initiatives/international-growth/international-player-pathway) was retrieved again and confirms 126 international-born roster participants, 61 active-roster participants and 38 countries/territories during 2025. Obtain a matching league cohort before converting these distinct counts into recruitment probabilities. Preserve birthplace, upbringing, ancestry and racial self-report separately; country context must allow locally born diaspora and mixed backgrounds.

A directly reviewed hair study found better agreement for six geometric groups than eight and classification differences among strands from one person ([primary study](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0172834)). **Implementation inference:** retain continuous curvature and bounded strand variation beneath texture labels; inspect intermediate shapes. The small selected sample does not set hairstyle frequencies. A historical 411-player NFL study measured regional composition with DXA ([author-institution abstract](https://experts.umn.edu/en/publications/body-composition-and-bone-mineral-density-of-national-football-le/)). Use it as a method-aware research input; resolve possible cohort overlap before pooling, and obtain compatible records before fitting surface anatomy.

The required delivery order is now explicit:

1. Build a versioned calibration artifact separating football recruitment, country/diaspora background, natural phenotype and grooming. Each conditional names its denominator, period, source definition and uncertainty. Preserve overlapping identities and mixed family origins. Do not reverse conditional probabilities or claim that separate margins establish a measured joint distribution.
2. Extend the saved person with individually addressable pigments, iris pattern, hair curvature/thickness/density/hairline, regional facial dimensions and asymmetry. Resolve cut, length, fade, part, braids/locs, facial hair, dye and treatment separately. Undisclosed identity must not select a fixed visual palette.
3. Fit the body around recorded stature, mass and measurement endpoints. Estimate absent shoulder/pelvis breadth, torso/leg proportions and regional bulk jointly, preserving compatible equal-height/weight differences. Record residuals and estimation provenance; measured outliers survive. Position can inform a supported cohort prior while a position change preserves the individual.
4. Apply separate athletic capacities through that body. Show a heavy, fast athlete and a light, slow athlete; demonstrate acceleration, cutting, jumping, strength and endurance independently. Weight/composition development is a dated physical event, while a capacity-only improvement preserves the frame.
5. Inspect actual-size roster/draft/dossier portraits plus common-scale front/side/body and all six motion views. Cover every legal hairstyle/texture pair, color/face channels, same-country diversity and mixed backgrounds, desktop/mobile and all themes. Require visible differences, coherent geometry and stable recognition after grooming, aging, trades and named-save replacement/load/reload.

Add three acceptance fixtures: incorrect conditional direction is refused; nearby curl values produce smooth visible changes rather than category jumps; incompatible composition methods or overlapping cohorts cannot be silently pooled. These extend the existing matrix. Research/plan completion does not establish runtime calibration or whole-player visual acceptance.

**Required physical-state implementation:** extend the existing saved measurement/identity authorities with dated mass, composition provenance, conditioning and training history. Generate a compatible initial body and capacities together when a fitted athlete cohort permits it, retaining individual variation and measured outliers. Reconcile recorded dimensions first; estimate absent dimensions with an explicit versioned model. A position change preserves the person. A training or weight-change event updates the dated physical state, resolves compatible regional bulk, then refreshes every portrait/body/motion view. A capacity-only change preserves anatomy. Keep observed drill results, evaluation ratings and animation calibration separately versioned.

The new [longitudinal athlete reference](PLAYER_PHYSICAL_DEVELOPMENT_REFERENCE_2026-10-07.json) adds a directly reviewed single-university study with 512 players and 2,628 observations over 15 years ([primary publisher](https://doi.org/10.1177/17479541251367154)). It supplies method-aware research input for development, rather than fitted NFL career or body-shape coefficients. The companion small linebacker study found no significant seasonal changes in composition or power ([primary publisher](https://journal.iusca.org/index.php/Journal/article/view/57)). **Implementation inference:** support individual trajectories and plateaus; do not force annual body inflation, muscle loss or a uniform rate of improvement.

Add three mandatory transition fixtures to the existing appearance matrix: (1) unchanged stature/reach and dated weight gain produces compatible bulk across front, side and motion; (2) unchanged weight with an observed composition change can redistribute regional build while conserving the model budget; (3) improved sprint/jump/strength capacity without new physical observations changes the corresponding demonstration while preserving the saved frame. Named-save replacement/load/reload must reproduce each transition. Missing composition is labeled estimated; a rating increase does not invent a measurement.

Current geometry follow-through: modern neutral and moving torsos now use curved cubic sidewalls that preserve the saved chest/waist sections and integrated artistic volume budget. Front/side legs expose the player's saved skin beneath a shared blue training kit; its hem follows the same hip-to-knee fraction used in movement. The initial rectangular waistband defect was found in browser captures and corrected with a curved yoke. Primary25/25 checks include numerical integration of the actual SVG curves, endpoint fidelity, six-mode hem placement and exact legacy rendering. Final comparison/dossier browser checks and both built runtime checks pass. Named restoration now includes an actual intervening MIA franchise replacement before reloading BUF. The [source-bound geometry/kit receipt](visual-qa/PLAYER_CURVED_TORSO_KIT_2026-10-07.json) records independent PASS:17 tests,54 actual cubic volume/positivity probes,252 exact legacy renders,216 unchanged poses and eight matched originals. The complete athlete still needs face/hair/form naturalism, empirical fitting and final whole-project acceptance.

Fresh [whole-person dossier inspection](visual-qa/PLAYER_WHOLE_PERSON_INSPECTION_2026-10-07.json) confirms that broader craft work is still required. The current server-runtime check passes identity/frame preservation and captures24 desktop/mobile dark/light states; five images were explicitly inspected. Open visual judgments include doll-like eye/cheek/mouth form, weak relief on darker skin, angular repeated long curls, cap-like afro volume and mannequin-like limb/hand presentation. Rework coherent facial anatomy and hair masses together, then reconcile compatible regional body form in front/side/motion at common scale. Retain measured endpoints and individual recipes. A mathematically conserved artistic volume is insufficient visual acceptance; weight alone cannot establish body fat.

Earlier material follow-through: shared diffuse skin and matte cloth materials reduce limb/trouser glare, and the modern jersey's artificial chest line is removed. Primary body/motion checks pass24/24; controlled comparison and actual dossier browser checks pass, as do built worker/in-page save/load/reload checks for all six movement modes. The [source-bound material receipt](visual-qa/PLAYER_BODY_LIGHTING_2026-10-07.json) records independent PASS for that scope only. It identified flatter moving limbs, rigid torso geometry and inconsistent front/side/motion clothing. The geometry/kit update above addresses the latter two findings; flatter form and complete naturalism remain open.

The required result is a visibly varied league: hairstyles, hair colors and textures, skin shades and undertones, eye colors, facial structure, facial hair, hairlines and body frames. Each player has one persistent individual recipe shared by portrait, profile, full-body and motion views. This section adds concrete requirements to the existing comparison matrix; it does not mark the overhaul complete.

**Generation order:** football recruitment context → birthplace/upbringing/family origins and source-defined identity responses → correlated individual natural appearance → grooming and dated aging state → measurement-constrained skeleton and regional build → athletic movement. Use separate random streams for appearance and football outcomes. National resident proportions inform compatible background context; NFL roster/entrant observations inform league recruitment. Neither supplies a complete appearance distribution. Keep mixed backgrounds and within-country variation visible.

| Work package | Concrete implementation requirement | Required acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Hair as geometry and material | Store curvature, strand thickness, density, hairline and natural pigment separately from cut, length, part, arrangement, dye and gray fraction. Add dated temple recession and crown density independently of shaving. Grooming can conceal natural characteristics without deleting them. | Same-person cut changes; same-cut texture/thickness/density changes; shaved versus naturally thinning examples; front/side consistency and readable normal-size results. |
| Complete face and pigmentation | Separate skin shade/undertone, iris pigment/pattern, scalp/brow/beard pigment and regional face/ear dimensions. Maintain overlapping, correlated combinations with individual residual variation. | Controlled pairs for each channel, then complete same-country and mixed-background people in roster, draft and dossier surfaces. A changed SVG identifier is not a visible trait change. |
| Physique from measurements | Constrain stature, reach, arm and hand endpoints first. Fit torso/leg shares, shoulder/pelvis breadth and regional bulk to recorded dimensions and dated mass; report missing measurements and fitting residuals. Preserve estimated muscle/fat allocation separately from measured composition. | Same height/different mass; same mass/different height; same height/mass/different compatible proportions; long/short reach; compact-heavy and tall-light cases at a common neutral scale. |
| Athletic expression through that body | Distinguish absolute measured drill results, any size-adjusted evaluation and gameplay ratings. Drive all six movement modes from the corresponding capacities through the saved frame. Dated conditioning/weight changes update physical state explicitly. | Heavy-fast/light-slow examples; individual speed, acceleration, agility, jump, strength and endurance counterfactuals; stable bones, plausible contact and reach through each motion. |
| Career continuity | Save recipe/version and dated presentation/physical changes. Existing identities retain their decoder; compatible imports retain units, endpoints and precision. | Actual grooming, trade, annual progression and named-save restoration preserve identity and measurements across every view. |

New evidence adds two specific implementation constraints. [MedlinePlus hair texture](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/hairtexture/) describes distinct influences on curvature and strand thickness, with additional age and chemical-treatment effects. [Its patterned-hair-loss reference](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/condition/androgenetic-alopecia/) distinguishes temple recession from crown thinning and describes variable onset. **Implementation inference:** use separate saved controls for these effects; record any player-age onset/progression probabilities as artistic assumptions until an appropriate cohort supports them. Do not turn hair loss into a medical condition or football-rating modifier.

The [2015 Combine normalization study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25436631/) examines 4,603 participants from 1999–2014 across six tests. Its abstract reports that simply dividing performance by mass can reverse the size association, while fitted allometric parameters vary by test and position. **Implementation inference:** do not equate a heavy player's absolute strength or sprint result with a universally normalized capacity, and do not infer musculature from one rating. Obtain the full parameter tables and validate on a separate compatible cohort before adopting coefficients. This historical complete-test sample is not today's NFL population, a joint body-shape distribution or a motion-capture reference.

Demographic calibration must also preserve classification changes. [The Census Bureau's standards update](https://www.census.gov/about/our-research/race-ethnicity/standards-updates.html) describes the 2024 combined, multiple-response standard and a separate Middle Eastern or North African category, with planned 2027 ACS/2030 Census implementation. Retain the original collection schema on older US/NFL observations; do not silently recode old categories into new ones or normalize overlapping responses to an exclusive 100-percent draw. Publish observed versus assumed status for every fitted weight.

Completion requires an evidence row per advertised trait and an inspected complete-player gallery, including desktop/mobile and both themes. Source research, numerical uniqueness and previously accepted component refinements remain separate from full visual acceptance.

## Concrete acceptance for the complete player — latest clarification

### Every visible trait and the measured athlete — required comparison matrix

Treat the requested variation as observable behavior in the game. Use matched comparison pairs to verify each independently addressable channel below. Keep the same camera, lighting, pose and other controls for each pair, then inspect the complete people in ordinary roster and dossier views.

| Comparison | Required visible result | Authority preserved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Same natural person, different haircut or arrangement | Distinct silhouette, part, length and strand treatment; corresponding front/side hairline and volume | Natural pigment, texture, facial landmarks and frame |
| Same cut, different supported texture/density/thickness | Visible strand curvature and distribution at normal portrait size | Cut, face and pigment; legal combinations recorded explicitly |
| Same anatomy, different scalp/brow/beard/iris/skin channel | The selected channel visibly changes; skin includes undertone and iris includes within-color variation | Other natural channels, geometry and measurements |
| Same country of birth, different people | Multiple backgrounds and visibly distinct combinations of face, hair, skin and eyes | Birthplace remains separate from family origins and self-identification |
| Same measured height, different mass | Compatible regional bulk and silhouette in front and side views | Stature and recorded limb/reach endpoints |
| Same height and weight, different individual frame | Different compatible shoulder/pelvis breadth and segment allocation, without a generic position silhouette | Recorded dimensions and mass; estimated composition labeled |
| Same frame, different athletic capacity | The corresponding speed, acceleration, cut, jump, strength or fatigue behavior changes | Skeleton, saved natural appearance and dated physical state |
| Same person after trade, grooming, aging and save/load | Changes persist across portrait, profile, body and motion; unchanged traits remain recognizable | Stable person identity, measurement history and recipe versions |

Report dimension residuals in their stored units and precision, and test neutral geometry before animation. Keep estimated muscle/fat allocation separate from measured weight: height and weight alone do not identify body composition. The [CDC anthropometry report](https://stacks.cdc.gov/view/cdc/174595) supplies population reference measurements, while its [protocol](https://wwwn.cdc.gov/nchs/data/nhanes/public/2021/manuals/2021-Anthropometry-Procedures-Manual-508.pdf) defines endpoints; neither supplies NFL-specific composition or joint proportions. Fit additional athlete observations before claiming those distributions.

Use background context for probabilistic representation with variation inside each context. The [National Human Genome Research Institute](https://www.genome.gov/About-Genomics/Introduction-to-Genomics) describes multiple genetic and environmental influences on individual traits; birthplace and a race label cannot replace a complete individual recipe. Do not calibrate physical ratings from pigment or racial labels. Record unsupported joint distributions as creative assumptions until compatible observations support them.

Additional pigmentation evidence covers 2,092 participants in Ethiopia, Tanzania and Botswana measured by inner-arm reflectance ([NIH research account](https://www.genome.gov/news/news-release/New-regions-of-the-human-genome-linked-to-skin-color-variation-in-some-African-populations), [original study](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5759959/)). Retain the study's heterogeneous populations and measurement method when extracting observations; obtain group sample sizes and joint coverage before fitting. Its cohort is not a national probability sample or an NFL cohort, and reflectance is not a renderer RGB value. This adds a research lead for within-background skin variation, with no new runtime coefficients adopted.

Completion requires the comparison matrix, legal grooming coverage, common-scale frame checks, all six movement demonstrations and actual career persistence evidence. A passing uniqueness count or a small facial-material improvement does not close this requirement.

The [bounded facial-relief receipt](visual-qa/PLAYER_FACE_RELIEF_2026-10-07.json) now records clearer nasal/lip relief and revised ear projection, with unchanged saved identity recipes. Nineteen focused tests and two built-browser persistence checks pass; the [independent component review](visual-qa/PLAYER_FACE_RELIEF_REVIEW_2026-10-07.json) passes after 21 tests, 96 legacy-output comparisons and inspection of 10 matched originals. The primary inspection covers 36 of 64 captures. Full facial naturalism, coherent hair material, body craft and the comparison matrix remain open; the visual gate still fails on that recorded quality finding.

The newer [matte torso receipt](visual-qa/PLAYER_TORSO_MATTE_2026-10-07.json) binds the current `6fb8c9a5257a` build. The shared garment material now uses softer diffuse light, faint weave and folds inside the exact existing frame envelope. Six controlled comparison groups cover mass, stature, individual proportions, capacities, heavy-fast/light-slow and recorded arm/reach differences; the capacity group renders all six posed movement modes. Actual generated dossiers preserve appearance/frame records, and both built runtimes pass named-save/load/reload followed by selecting all six modes. Twenty-four focused tests and eight [independent checks](visual-qa/PLAYER_TORSO_MATTE_REVIEW_2026-10-07.json) pass; the primary review inspects 32 of 96 captures. Static front/side comparisons share a neutral scale, while motion uses a different camera. Timed behavior and source measurement methods remain distinct from posed images. The rigid torso envelope, shiny limb/trouser material and broader face/hair quality remain open; this material change does not establish empirical body composition or complete player acceptance.

### Correlated individuality — latest research addition

The [extracted primary pigmentation reference](PLAYER_PIGMENT_JOINT_REFERENCE_2026-10-07.json) adds measured joint-trait evidence: hair/eye correlation 0.50, hair/skin 0.30 and eye/skin 0.31 in one volunteer cohort. It supports separate correlated channels, with no runtime frequencies adopted ([publisher](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-018-08147-0)).

Implement this through the existing saved identity authority, with independently addressable natural scalp, brow, beard, iris and skin channels. Record the calibration version and creative assumptions beside each fitted distribution. Preserve continuous variation inside named color categories: dark eyes must still differ in hue/chroma/lightness; dark hair in pigment and material; skin in shade and undertone. Keep lighting and display-theme effects outside the natural recipe. A haircut or dye change must preserve natural pigment, facial landmarks and the saved frame.

Add explicit acceptance fixtures for a brunette with light eyes, different iris shades within the brown category, contrasting beard/scalp pigment, and same-country players with different skin/hair/eye combinations. These are supported rendering capabilities, not claims about their population prevalence. Review one-channel changes with identical camera, illumination and grooming, then review complete people under normal game lighting. Measure distinguishability at the actual roster and dossier sizes rather than enlarging only the diagnostic images.

For the body, use one saved constraint solution across portrait, front, side and motion. The solver must expose stature, reach and mass residuals separately from artistic surface choices. At equal stature and weight, retain long-limbed/narrower and shorter-limbed/broader compatible individuals; at equal anatomy, vary athletic timing without changing their frame. A conditioning or weight event updates dated physical state, with an explicit transition to compatible regional bulk. Verify standing scale, limb endpoints, joint continuity and foot contact before assessing the face/body's visual quality. These fixtures supplement the measured-frame and six-capacity requirements below.

The actual [22-season built-browser persistence diagnostic](MANAGED_BROWSER_STORAGE_GAP_2026-10-07.json) records the original worker and in-page failures when saving the9.65MB checkpoint: IndexedDB open timeout, permanent localStorage degradation and storage-full failure. The bounded transient-timeout repair is now integrated, with25 portable fault/atomicity checks and a passing Pages build. The [production browser proof](HYBRID_TIMEOUT_PRODUCTION_2026-10-07.json) passes in worker and in-page modes: Settings named save → replace the franchise → load → reload preserves all9,213 persisted person recipes, raw physical records and resolved appearance/frame/measurements. Final independent integration review passes with scope limits. This22-season checkpoint proof does not replace final30-season, all-feature, visual or country-calibration acceptance. Preserve the original failure evidence.

Deliver visibly different people across haircuts, natural/dyed hair, skin shades and undertones, iris colors, facial anatomy, facial hair and compatible body frames. Each trait must be visible in ordinary game views and survive saving. The implementation must include:

- **Background sampling:** retain country of birth, upbringing, family origins and source-defined identity responses separately. Fit the football recruitment cohort first, then compatible country context. Add the [six actual New Zealand census ethnicity observations](PLAYER_NZ_ETHNIC_OBSERVATIONS_2026-10-07.json): these are overlapping responses, with Māori descent recorded separately. Their percentages total 114.8%, so normalizing them into exclusive race probabilities would destroy their meaning. Obtain compatible combination and birthplace observations before using them for mixed-background fitting ([Stats NZ definitions](https://datainfoplus.stats.govt.nz/Item/nz.govt.stats/1f1ca131-a9aa-483b-98ab-d22bf84ae944)).
- **Visible traits:** support distinct natural skin, iris, scalp, beard and brow pigment; texture, strand thickness, density and hairline; regional facial structure and asymmetry; plus independent cut, length, part, arrangement, dye and graying. Persist the natural person and mutable grooming separately. Hair texture and thickness have several influences, including age and treatment; do not use one racial category to select all hair geometry ([MedlinePlus](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/hairtexture/)).
- **Measured frame:** reconstruct height, arm length and wingspan using consistent measurement endpoints. Allocate neck, shoulder, chest, waist, pelvis, upper/lower limb, hand and foot geometry through stable individual proportions. Weight influences compatible regional bulk; equal height/weight must permit visibly different frames. Preserve dated measurements and label missing anatomy/composition estimates. Validate dimensions against stored measurement precision before pose, then check the silhouette at a common scale.
- **Athletic presentation:** provide separate speed, acceleration, cutting, jumping, strength and endurance demonstrations. Check foot contact, stride, joint placement, reach and effort through the same body. Include heavy-fast and light-slow players. Rating changes affect the appropriate behavior; dated body changes need their own physical-state authority.
- **Whole-player proof:** inspect every legal hairstyle/texture pair and advertised color/face/body channel at normal portrait size, front/side/full body and motion, desktop/mobile and both themes. Use one-trait comparison pairs, same-country different-person groups, mixed-background cases, equal-height mass contrasts and equal-height/weight frame contrasts. Actual trade, grooming, aging and named-save/reload commands must preserve identity. Record concrete rendering defects and fix them before acceptance.

These are required delivery checks. Census margins are context evidence; empirical trait frequencies, whole-person visual quality and complete athletic demonstrations remain open. Existing scoped hair/face/body refinements do not establish completion.

### Body proportions — concrete additional research and implementation

The [four extracted limb-reference rows](PLAYER_LIMB_REFERENCE_OBSERVATIONS_2026-10-07.json) preserve measured upper-arm and upper-leg distributions for men aged 20–29 and 30–39 from the [2021–2023 NHANES report](https://www.cdc.gov/nchs/data/series/sr_03/sr03-050.pdf). These general-population marginals help check segment variation; they cannot fit NFL frames, height-conditioned proportions or joint covariance. The [body-measures documentation](https://wwwn.cdc.gov/Nchs/Data/Nhanes/Public/2021/DataFiles/BMX_L.htm) supplies eligibility, missingness, comment codes and examination-weight requirements. Keep upper-arm length distinct from full arm length and wingspan, and reconcile measurement endpoints before rendering.

The [measurement protocol](https://wwwn.cdc.gov/nchs/data/nhanes/public/2021/manuals/2021-Anthropometry-Procedures-Manual-508.pdf) also reveals an important mapping issue: these arm and thigh measurements follow external landmarks and tape paths. They are not directly interchangeable with the renderer's shoulder/elbow or hip/knee joint distances. Preserve the method and require a validated conversion before using these values as bone lengths.

Extend the existing measurement and geometry authorities in `src/domain/playerMeasurements.js`, `src/domain/playerVisualIdentity.js` and `public/lib/physiqueGeometryContract.js`. Their saved individual anatomy and regional allocation already support variation, but the fixed 1 kg/litre volume proxy and fictional proportion ranges still require external validation. Preserve those versioned saved recipes while improving the solver: recorded stature fixes total neutral height; recorded reach and limb lengths constrain their endpoints; stable individual proportions distribute the remaining segments. Fit compatible regional mass around that skeleton, with separately estimated shoulders, neck, chest, waist, pelvis, arms and legs. Avoid converting a circumference into a diameter without an explicit cross-section assumption. Surface estimates as estimates. Where constraints conflict, report the residual and preserve the original measurement rather than silently changing it.

Make the physique compatible with the athlete's measured performance and dated training/conditioning state. Research athlete-cohort relationships before adding correlations among muscle distribution, mass and capacity. Strength, speed or potential changes alone must not reroll bone lengths or invent measured body composition; explicit physical-development events update dated mass/build consistently across portraits, body views and movement.

Acceptance must include equal-height/weight players with different compatible proportions, equal-height players at different weights, long/short arms at matched stature, and heavy-fast/light-slow cases. Inspect these at one common neutral scale in front and side views, then verify reach, foot contact and joint continuity in all six movement modes. No source here establishes racial body templates, muscle composition or football talent.

Include a heavy-fast reference fixture: 311 pounds, 4.91-second 40-yard dash ([2026 Combine report](https://www.nfl.com/news/2026-nfl-combine-spencer-fano-expresses-openness-to-position-change-after-arms-measure-32-1-8-inches)). One observation provides no league frequency or rating conversion.

### Recruitment evidence update — checked October 7, 2026

[NFL Football Operations](https://edge-operations.nfl.com/programs-initiatives/international-growth/international-player-pathway) reports 126 international-born players on rosters during the 2025 regular season and playoffs, including 61 on active rosters, representing 38 countries and territories. These are season-wide roster observations with distinct denominators, not an opening-day percentage. Preserve the source's country/territory coverage and roster definitions; obtain the matching complete player cohort before deriving recruitment probabilities. Heritage-program participation is a separate measure and must not substitute for birthplace, ancestry or racial self-identification. The existing fictional international recruitment share remains an assumption until this reconciliation is complete.

Implement overlapping, individually varied appearance distributions inside each background context. Natural hair and iris pigmentation use separate correlated channels, with dye, grooming and aging stored separately; [MedlinePlus hair-color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/haircolor/) and [eye-color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/) references describe multiple genetic influences rather than a single categorical appearance switch. Neither these references nor roster birthplace counts establish country-specific trait frequencies. Compare generated skin shades, iris colors, hair colors/textures and facial variation within the same country as well as across the whole football cohort. Measurable-constrained frames and individually varied regional bulk remain mandatory, alongside separate athletic demonstrations.

### Trait authority and career continuity — additional concrete requirements

| Layer | Required variation and authority | Acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Family/background | Birthplace, upbringing, multiple family origins and source-defined identity responses; compatible NFL cohort and country observations | Preserve original denominators and missing/undisclosed responses; show multiple distinct people from the same country and mixed/diaspora cases |
| Natural appearance | Continuous skin shade/undertone; distinct iris, scalp, brow and beard pigment; hair curvature, density, thickness and hairline; regional facial anatomy/asymmetry | One-trait comparison pairs under identical lighting; every advertised channel visible at normal portrait size |
| Grooming | Cut, length, part, arrangement, braids/locs, scalp/facial hair, dye and graying | Legal combinations remain coherent across front/side/body views; grooming changes preserve the person's natural recipe |
| Measured skeleton | Stature, arm length, wingspan and recorded hand dimensions, each with units, date and measurement endpoints | Compare neutral geometry with the stored measurement precision; scaling/posing cannot silently change the measurement |
| Regional build | Neck, shoulders, chest, waist, pelvis, arms, thighs, calves, hands and feet; compatible mass and individual residual proportions | Common-scale tall/short, light/heavy and equal-height/weight different-frame cases; estimates remain distinct from measured composition |
| Athletic behavior | Speed, acceleration, agility, jumping, strength and endurance through the same saved body | Independent rating counterfactuals, heavy-fast/light-slow examples, plausible foot contact and joint/reach continuity |
| Career changes | Explicit dated weight/conditioning observations and separately saved grooming/aging state | Actual career commands and named-save restoration preserve identity, measurement history and resolved proportions; loading never rerolls a person |

Add a versioned, individual aging recipe for graying rather than a universal birthday switch. A [primary worldwide survey of4,192 volunteers](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22716034/) found heterogeneous graying and challenged the usual50/50/50 rule. Its reported45–65-year cohort is not an NFL-player age calibration; no annual graying rates or country weights are adopted. Any young-player graying assumptions require an explicit creative-assumption entry and visual checks. Keep natural pigment, gray fraction and dye separate, so dye does not overwrite the inherited person.

If temporary complexion changes are added, preserve baseline skin pigment separately from the dated presentation state. [MedlinePlus describes tanning as increased melanin after ultraviolet exposure](https://medlineplus.gov/tanning.html); this supports the distinction, not a country-by-skin distribution or an automatic season/weather mechanic. Athletic ability, intelligence, personality and injury tolerance must use their own evidence and individual generation inputs rather than race/skin/eye/hair labels. Population identity categories are source-defined social responses ([Census definitions](https://www.census.gov/topics/population/race/about/faq.html)).

## Visible individual variation — required delivery sequence

Additional country-born context now includes [27 actual New Zealand birthplace × ethnicity cells](PLAYER_NZ_BIRTH_ETHNIC_OBSERVATIONS_2026-10-07.json), with nine consistent total birthplace rows from the official2023 summaries. The published New Zealand-born count is3,525,744; overlapping responses include214,458 Asian,293,583 Pacific Peoples and854,220 Māori. Retain these separate indicators, their source categories, missingness and rounding. The previously extracted full-resident margins cannot replace these country-born counts. Neither supplies an exclusive race draw or NFL recruitment/appearance frequencies. Extraction and arithmetic have independent scoped review; single/combination and age/sex observations remain required before background fitting. Sources: [Asian](https://tools.summaries.stats.govt.nz/ethnic-group/asian), [Pacific Peoples](https://tools.summaries.stats.govt.nz/ethnic-group/pacific-peoples), [Māori](https://tools.summaries.stats.govt.nz/ethnic-group/maori).

Implement the user's complete appearance requirement through one persistent person shared by portraits, side views, full-body profiles and animation. Birthplace influences researched background probabilities; it never selects a single race or appearance. Keep ancestry, birthplace, upbringing and source-defined race/ethnicity responses distinct, with mixed and diaspora backgrounds and substantial variation within every country.

1. Publish a versioned calibration table with source year, population, denominator, original category, mapped category, uncertainty and observed-versus-assumed status. Use NFL recruitment/roster observations for league composition and compatible national observations for family context. World population shares cannot replace football recruitment shares. The actual ONS joint counts and ABS multiple-response ancestry observations already extracted are context evidence, not national pigment frequencies. Unsupported joint appearance frequencies remain explicit creative assumptions.
2. Resolve separate natural skin/undertone, iris, scalp/beard/brow pigment and hair texture/density/hairline channels before resolving grooming. Render cuts, lengths, parts, braids, locs, dye, graying and facial hair visibly, preserving supported correlations without tying cuts or palettes to racial categories. Pigment channels are correlated individual traits rather than one interchangeable color index ([MedlinePlus eye-color research](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/), [hair-color research](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/haircolor/)).
3. Construct stature and limb endpoints from recorded measurements, then allocate compatible regional bulk from dated mass and saved estimated build. Include shoulders, neck, chest, waist, pelvis, upper/lower limbs, hands and feet; distinguish measured dimensions from estimates. Equal height/weight must still allow different compatible frames. Missing composition data must not become a claimed body-fat or muscle measurement.
4. Apply speed, acceleration, agility, jumping, strength and endurance to movement through that body. Preserve heavy-fast and light-slow cases. Athletic ratings cannot alone determine visible musculature or skeletal dimensions; conditioning updates must preserve identity and measurement provenance.
5. Require actual-size front/side/body/motion galleries for every advertised trait and legal hairstyle/texture combination, controlled single-trait pairs, common-scale body extremes, representative same-country/mixed backgrounds and named-save career continuity. Record clipping, distinguishability and consistency findings. Numerical uniqueness, metadata coverage and passing renderer tests do not replace inspection of the actual players.

This sequence is mandatory implementation and acceptance work, not a claim that the complete overhaul is finished. The NFL's [international roster report](https://edge-operations.nfl.com/programs-initiatives/international-growth/international-player-pathway) also distinguishes full-season roster participation from active-roster and pathway cohorts; calibration must keep those denominators separate.

## Latest appearance requirement: visible variation and research adoption

The user requires different hairstyles, hair colors, skin colors, eye colors and individual anatomy throughout the league, with body frames and proportions matching each player's measurables and athletic behavior. This is a release acceptance requirement for the overhaul, not an optional cosmetic expansion. Existing numeric trait diversity and the illustrated renderer do not satisfy it on their own.

| Deliverable | Required behavior | Acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Population and background | Sample the football recruitment cohort, then compatible country/upbringing/family contexts. Preserve mixed identities and source-specific reporting categories. Players born in the same country must still vary substantially. | Compatible dated league margins, country-conditioned diagnostics, and reviewed diaspora/mixed cases; no team quotas or race-derived football attributes. |
| Hair and grooming | Render distinct cut silhouettes, straight/wavy/curly/coiled geometry, density, hairline, natural black/brown/blond/red shades, dye, partial graying, beard and brow variation. Cuts depend on physical length/texture/treatment constraints rather than racial locks. | Every supported legal cut/texture combination inspected at actual dossier size; paired natural-versus-dyed, shaved-versus-bald, sparse-versus-dense and scalp-versus-beard cases. |
| Skin, eyes and facial identity | Continuous skin shade/undertone and iris shade, plus independently varied facial regions. Use consistent lighting across head, hands and body; preserve iris readability without enlarging eyes or caricaturing anatomy. | Equal-lighting pigment pairs, same-background different-person galleries, recognizable front/side/moving views and grooming-only continuity. |
| Body shape | Height fixes stature; recorded limb/reach measurements constrain their correct endpoints. Dated weight constrains compatible bulk, while regional build and missing proportions retain individually varied estimates. | Common-scale equal-height/different-weight and equal-height/weight/different-proportions pairs; independent stature/reach reconstruction and unclipped extremes. |
| Athletic presentation | Separate speed, acceleration, cutting, jumping, strength and endurance influence movement through the saved frame. Heavy-fast and light-slow players remain possible. | Matched anatomy with contrasting capacities, joint/ground/reach consistency and dated conditioning changes without skeletal rerolls. |
| Persistence | One saved person across scouting, draft, roster, trade, aging and named-save replacement/load/reload. | Cosmetic changes preserve identity and football randomness; all advertised channels visibly affect the intended views. |

The [additional research extraction](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_RESEARCH_ADOPTION_2026-10-07.json) includes the Australian census's generation-conditioned ancestry table as a concrete example: birthplace alone loses substantial context, and its multiple-response ancestry percentages cannot become mutually exclusive race draws. It also rechecks natural pigment/texture distinctions, Combine composition evidence and the NFL's dated international roster population. These observations do not provide an empirical country-by-appearance generator.

**Research adoption order:** extract compatible observations and their denominators first; specify mappings and uncertainty second; validate fitted joint/residual distributions third; change versioned generation coefficients only after those checks. Keep the present 2023 league reference dated, and keep the 4% international-birth prior and country affinities labeled assumptions until replaced. Search did not establish a newer primary NFL demographic report in this review. Country context, population fitting and visual quality remain separate gates. The next empirical deliverable is usable observation rows/cross-tabs, not another metadata-only source list.

## Complete appearance contract — latest clarification

Different hair, skin, eyes, faces and body proportions are mandatory parts of the same saved player, including players born in the same country and playing the same position. The [trait authority manifest](PLAYER_TRAIT_AUTHORITY_2026-10-07.json) connects these requirements to the existing resolver files, research, provenance and concrete acceptance fixtures. It specifies planned logical channels; it does not claim those fields or renderers are complete.

Implement natural scalp pigment, beard/brow pigment, iris pigment and skin pigment/undertone as separately addressable channels with supported correlations. Store natural curl geometry, strand thickness, density and hairline separately from current cut, length, arrangement, treatment, dye and facial-hair grooming. Include facial structure, ear/nose/lip shapes and asymmetry, hand/foot dimensions, neck, shoulders, pelvis, torso/leg balance and regional limb/trunk build. Keep unsupported frequencies explicitly versioned artistic assumptions. Natural appearance must remain recognizable after grooming changes.

Build the skeleton from recorded standing height and source-defined limb/reach measurements before allocating compatible soft-tissue volume from dated weight and available composition observations. Clothing and pose must not conceal whether those dimensions agree. Estimated anatomy needs saved individual residuals and provenance. The current fixed-density volume proxy remains artistic until validated; it must not be presented as measured body fat or muscle mass. Preserve supported outliers instead of silently forcing positional averages.

Athleticism must be visible through separate speed, acceleration, cutting, jumping, strength and endurance behavior. These capacities influence motion through the player's skeleton; they do not independently assign shoulder width, musculature or body fat. Dated conditioning/composition changes may alter regional build while height and skeletal identity stay consistent. The [paired 2026 collegiate DXA study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41369529/) supports keeping regional composition separate from total mass, but supplies no adopted weekly or NFL career rates.

Acceptance must include the manifest's paired fixtures at actual portrait size and common body scale: grooming-only changes; iris-only changes; skin undertone under equal lighting; equal height/different weight; equal height and weight/different compatible proportions; equal anatomy/different capacities; and saved career continuity. Check each legal style/texture combination and all shared views across desktop/mobile and themes. Population fitting and visual recognition are separate gates: diverse numeric recipes cannot establish either on their own.

## Appearance delivery sequence — latest user clarification

Every generated player must resolve to an individually recognizable person with a compatible measured body. The following sequence makes the existing research requirements executable; these tasks remain pending until their acceptance evidence passes.

1. **Fit the recruitment cohort before appearance.** Keep birth country, childhood football location, citizenship, family origins and source-defined race/ethnicity responses separate. Use dated NFL roster/draft observations for league representation and compatible country or diaspora observations for conditional backgrounds. Retain multiple identities and unknown responses. World population shares alone cannot set NFL recruitment shares. No race-derived ratings, potential, intelligence, temperament or injury modifiers.
2. **Save natural identity independently of grooming.** Extend the existing versioned recipe with continuous skin pigment/undertone, natural hair and iris pigment, curl geometry, strand thickness, density, hairline, eyebrow and beard characteristics, and regional facial geometry. Permit individual variation and overlapping appearances within every background. Preserve supported correlations without selecting one complete face/palette from nationality. Where compatible trait frequencies are absent, record explicit artistic priors rather than invented statistics.
3. **Save a separate mutable grooming state.** Implement shaved/buzz, cropped, fades, straight/wavy/curly/coiled short and long hair, afros, braids, cornrows, locs, tied-back hair, receding/sparse hair and baldness with appropriate geometry. Length, part, arrangement, dye, gray-fiber coverage and facial-hair grooming are independent controls. Compatibility rules prevent impossible arrangements, clipping and helmet-like hair. This is the supported style inventory, not a claim about national prevalence; style frequencies need their own evidence or labeled assumptions.
4. **Construct the frame from measurements.** Reconcile height, dated weight, arm length, wingspan and available hand/breadth measurements using their actual endpoints. Estimate missing torso/leg ratio, shoulder/hip breadth, chest/waist and limb volumes with stable individual residuals and provenance. Enforce total stature and plausible mass/volume relationships, without forcing all players at one position into one silhouette. Keep measured body composition separate from estimated composition; height/weight or bench repetitions cannot uniquely determine it.
5. **Animate capacities through that frame.** Speed, acceleration, cutting, jumping, strength and endurance each affect their appropriate movement cues. Preserve bone lengths and body dimensions. Heavy-fast, light-slow, long-limbed and compact powerful players are valid; a single athleticism scalar cannot overwrite anatomy. Cosmetics use a separate deterministic random stream and cannot change football outcomes.
6. **Ship through the shared renderer and save version.** Roster, scouting, draft, dossier, front/side and motion views consume the same resolved person. Preserve existing identities during migration. Aging changes grooming/pigment and dated conditioning state while retaining recognizable facial and skeletal identity; trades and reloads preserve everything.

Acceptance requires paired, actual-size renders: same person with changed cut/dye; different iris shades; different skin undertones under identical lighting; same height with different weight; same height/weight with different compatible proportions; and the same anatomy with different physical capacities. Inspect every legal hair-style/texture pair and visible face/body joins. Show bodies at a common scale as well as ordinary card crops. Review all touched surfaces on desktop/mobile in every theme. Check whole-league/draft distributions and within-team variation across seeds, plus saved-player continuity; numerical recipe uniqueness alone cannot establish visual individuality.

### Additional recruitment evidence reviewed October 7, 2026

The [NFL's current international-player review](https://operations.nfl.com/programs-initiatives/international-growth/international-player-pathway) distinguishes 126 international-born players across 2025 regular-season/playoff rosters (61 on active rosters), 20 IPP players entering Week1 2026, and 318 participating players in the 2026 Heritage program. These are different cohorts and dates. None supplies a complete same-date league denominator or country-by-race/pigment joint table; do not divide these counts by nominal roster capacity or equate heritage with birthplace. Treat them as extraction targets and plausibility checks, not replacement sampling weights.

The [Census Bureau's 2020 reporting analysis](https://www.census.gov/library/stories/2021/08/improved-race-ethnicity-measures-reveal-united-states-population-much-more-multiracial.html) reports 10.2% multiple-race responses and explains questionnaire/coding effects on comparisons. Preserve original multiple responses and methodology; a national all-age population margin cannot directly calibrate NFL players or phenotype.

The [2018 Combine study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30540279/) analyzes 5,506 participants from 2002–2016 and compares multiple drills by position and later awards. Its abstract supports separate physical capacities and position-specific analysis, not causal talent rules or renderable individual anatomy. Raw observations and externally validated fitting coefficients have not been adopted. These additions supplement the existing pigmentation, hair, body-composition and country evidence; empirical fitting remains required.

## Expanded individual appearance and measured-body requirements — 2026-10-07

The user's clarification makes complete visible individuality mandatory: different hairstyles, natural/dyed hair colors, skin shades, eye colors, faces and body frames. These are additional acceptance requirements for the existing packages, not evidence of completed implementation. The [expanded research review](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#individual-variation-measurement-methods-and-outliers--2026-10-07) and appearance ledger now cover 40 sources.

| Delivery | Required implementation | Acceptance evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Demographics | Preserve birth country, upbringing, family origins and source-defined identity responses separately. Fit compatible joint observations; distinguish NFL representation from world population and migration context. Birthplace must permit multiple backgrounds and appearances. | All 47 countries have a coverage matrix naming categories, year, cohort, denominators, missingness, joint evidence and fallback. Compare league/draft frequencies with compatible observations, uncertainty and assumption sensitivity. No per-team racial quotas. |
| Hair and grooming | Separate natural pigment, continuous curl geometry, strand thickness, density and hairline from cut, length, arrangement, dye and graying. Beard/brow pigment and grooming remain independent. | Inspect every supported legal style/texture pair at actual portrait size, including braids/locs, long/short cuts, shaved/receding, sparse hair, partial gray and dyed scalp with natural beard/brows. Correct helmet-like silhouettes and clipping. Hairstyle prevalence needs its own dated evidence or explicit design assumption. |
| Complexion, eyes and face | Independently addressable continuous skin pigment/undertone, iris pigment and regional facial geometry, with supported correlations and individual residuals. Separate reference lighting from scene lighting. | Paired renders isolate each channel. Inspect iris contrast at dossier size and recognition across front/side/moving views; do not enlarge tiny roster eyes simply to claim visible differences. Recheck fixed complexion across themes and lighting. |
| Skeleton and build | Recorded height, dated weight, arm/reach and hand dimensions constrain geometry before estimated girths, torso/leg balance and composition. Preserve measurement endpoints and methods. Position informs a distribution with individual variation. | Common-scale views show tall/light, short/heavy, equal-height/different-mass and equal-height/mass/different-proportion frames. Preserve valid measured outliers. Report reconstruction error separately from artistic plausibility; validate held-out observations before calling geometry calibrated. |
| Athletic movement | Separate speed, acceleration, direction change, jumping, strength and endurance. Apply appropriate movement timing, displacement and effort with measured/estimated provenance; preserve the recorded skeleton. | Compare equal frames with different capacities, plus heavy-fast/light-slow cases. Verify planted feet, limb reach, joint bounds and no frame inflation. Drills and observed game motion remain distinct; strength alone cannot assign muscle mass or body-fat percentage. |
| Career continuity | Preserve natural appearance and skeletal identity through trades, drafts, reloads and aging. Dated weight/composition changes update compatible body volume; grooming and aging have separate state. | Actual saved-player journeys remain recognizable through cuts, dye, graying and plausible weight changes. Cosmetic changes preserve football outcomes, ratings and simulation randomness. Versioned decoding preserves existing saves. |

Implement in dependency order: source coverage and joint extraction; saved channels and constrained body model; shared portrait/body/motion renderers; actual-size review and career persistence. Audit existing resolvers before adding parallel traits or another complete cosmetic preset. New papers do not by themselves justify new numerical sampling weights.

The [verified ACS metadata crosswalk](PLAYER_ACS_CROSSWALK_2026-10-07.json) provides candidate source codes for all 47 supported countries and preserves separate race/Hispanic-origin/ancestry definitions. It passes structural checks and independent metadata review. One broader `Korea` recode remains explicitly qualified; UK subcountry/unspecified reporting and U.S. territories remain explicit. Use this artifact to constrain observation extraction, not to claim demographic fitting: person records, compatible cross-tabs, uncertainty and runtime calibration remain unverified.

Use `src/domain/playerVisualIdentity.js` as the saved recipe/version authority, `src/domain/playerMeasurements.js` as the measurement authority, and `src/domain/playerIdentity.js` as the shared appearance/frame resolver. Extend their existing channels rather than creating separate roster, dossier and animation identities. Preserve legacy recipe decoders. Replace assumed demographic and pigment weights only after source extraction and validation; documenting a source is not calibration.

The body resolver must distinguish measured inputs from estimated anatomy: total standing height constrains segment sums, source-defined arm/reach endpoints constrain compatible breadth and limbs, and dated mass constrains plausible regional volume. Hand span is not hand length. Resolve contradictory measurements explicitly instead of silently clipping them into a position template. Height/weight alone cannot uniquely determine body fat, torso/leg balance, shoulders, hips, chest, waist, arms or thighs; save compatible individual residuals and their assumption version. Athletic capacities then drive stride timing, acceleration, jumping, cutting and exertion through the same skeleton. A high strength or speed rating must not automatically enlarge the player or overwrite recorded measurements.

The review set must isolate one change at a time: same player with a different haircut/dye; same height with different mass; same height/mass with different observed proportions; same anatomy with different athletic capacities; and a saved player before/after a dated weight change. Inspect common-scale bodies as well as normal portrait crops, so automatic fit-to-card scaling cannot hide stature differences. Include hands, feet, hair silhouettes, eyebrows, facial hair, skin undertones and iris shades; inspect head/neck/shoulder joins and front/side/motion consistency. These are completion conditions for the full overhaul, not claims that existing illustrated assets pass.

Inspect roster, scouting, draft and dossier surfaces on desktop/mobile in every supported theme and leave a source-bound visual receipt. Numeric recipe diversity cannot substitute for recognizable people. Empirical demographic fitting and visible quality remain separate required gates.

### Concrete resolver changes required by the latest clarification

The current code already stores substantial variation, but the following source-level gaps remain. These are implementation tasks, not completed calibration.

| Current authority | Required change | Completion check |
|---|---|---|
| `playerIdentity.js`: `PIGMENTATION_ART_PRIORS` / `weightedPalette` | Replace new-generation artistic hair/eye weights and the fixed complexion-dependent multiplier only when compatible observations support a fitted model. Keep overlapping support, individual variation, source scope and an explicit fallback. Preserve frozen legacy decoders. | Report the adopted joint model, held-out errors and assumption sensitivity; compare same-background individuals and mixed backgrounds. Census identity responses alone do not establish pigment frequencies. |
| `playerIdentity.js`: `playerFrame` | Reconcile recorded stature, mass, reach, arms and breadth before geometry resolution. Existing visual clamps and fallback shoulders must report when they differ from a recorded observation; avoid silently presenting the fallback as measured anatomy. | Include valid outliers and contradictory measurement fixtures. The measurement display preserves the original value, while the rendered estimate exposes its reconciliation and bounded residual. |
| `playerMeasurements.js`: `resolveBodyVolume` | Calibrate compatible regional volumes and surface proportions using appropriate body observations. Its current fixed artistic density proxy remains an assumption; retain separate dated mass and stable skeletal proportions. | Compare equal-height/mass bodies with different torso/leg balance and regional bulk; record mass/segment reconstruction error separately from visual plausibility. |
| `playerIdentity.js` / `public/lib/playerMotion.js` | Modern static definition now uses a separately seeded artistic channel. Preserve it independently of strength; keep historical decoding stable. Six illustrative motion modes use the shared skeleton, with their timing assumptions requiring compatible workout/physiology calibration. | Equal-anatomy/different-capacity fixtures change the intended motion while preserving dimensions; heavy-fast and light-slow bodies remain valid. |
| Saved appearance and shared renderers | Complete readable hair fibers/silhouettes, complexion/undertones, iris shades and facial features across views, then verify actual players at normal interface size. | Inspect every style/texture combination and paired trait changes; reject values that are stored but have no perceptible effect at the appropriate viewing size. Save/load and career journeys preserve recognition. |

Research supports separating natural hair curvature/thickness from styling and treatment, and treating pigmentation as continuous and partly correlated rather than as a complete country preset. See the National Library of Medicine's [hair-texture](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/hairtexture/), [hair-color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/haircolor/) and [eye-color](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/) explanations. These sources inform the model structure; they do not supply country-by-trait or NFL sampling coefficients.

## Required demographic and appearance fitting contract — 2026-10-07

The latest request covers every visible player attribute. Complete the existing whole-player packages below using the expanded [35-source evidence ledger](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_EVIDENCE_2026-10-06.json) and [new joint-demographic/professional-build review](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#joint-demographic-evidence-and-professional-regional-build--2026-10-07). This is required implementation and acceptance work; research coverage alone does not complete it.

1. **Build a source crosswalk for all 47 supported birth countries.** Record geography, collection year, resident versus birth-country population, age/sex scope, original identity categories, single/multiple response mode, missingness and joint-table availability. Preserve the source responses alongside any display mapping. Use dated NFL cohorts to calibrate league representation; national census margins supply country context. Where athlete-specific joint evidence is absent, retain an explicitly versioned simulation assumption and report its sensitivity. Never silently normalize overlapping identity responses into exclusive probabilities.
2. **Sample a background before separate cosmetic channels.** Preserve birthplace, upbringing, family origins, mixed backgrounds and diaspora. Supported evidence may inform overlapping trait priors; it cannot select one complete skin/hair/eye/face bundle. Natural hair pigment, curvature, strand width, density and hairline remain distinct from haircut, length, styling, braids/locs, dye and graying. Give beard/brows separate controls. Keep continuous complexion/undertone and iris pigment, plus regional facial individuality. Unknown reporting must preserve a player's saved appearance.
3. **Fit compatible body inputs jointly.** Anchor measured height, mass, reach/arm and hand dimensions first, then resolve torso/leg balance, shoulder/pelvic breadth and regional volume. Separate observed composition, dated weight and stable estimates. Audit scanner exclusions and position counts before using historical NFL DXA observations; do not turn tissue averages into surface dimensions. Calibrate mass consistency and measurement endpoints before adopting numerical geometry coefficients. Position may inform a supported distribution, while individual residuals preserve different compatible builds at equal height/weight.
4. **Keep athletic capacity visible and distinct.** Measured drills, observed game motion and rating-derived estimates need separate provenance. Speed, acceleration, jumping, strength and direction change affect appropriate movement cues without changing recorded skeleton dimensions. Body fat cannot be calculated from race, birthplace, overall rating or speed. Include heavy-fast, light-slow and equal-frame/different-motion specimens.
5. **Verify the resulting league and the actual portraits.** Produce reproducible initial-roster, practice-squad, draft and undrafted cohort reports with source-compatible denominators, sampling uncertainty and within-background variation. Validate held-out measured body inputs separately from artistic estimates. Inspect paired fixtures at actual roster/scouting/draft/dossier sizes and common-scale front/side/motion views on desktop/mobile in both themes. Verify saved recognition through grooming, trades, reloads and career changes; changing cosmetics alone must preserve football results and simulation randomness.

Acceptance artifacts must name adopted coefficients, their source and fit error; record unsupported weights as assumptions; and distinguish measured accuracy from visual quality. Do not force per-team racial quotas or use a birth country as an individual racial assignment. Existing fictional weights and illustrated rendering remain partial until this contract passes.

### Concrete demographic extraction and whole-appearance acceptance

Current [generated cohort diagnostics](PLAYER_COHORT_DIAGNOSTICS_2026-10-07.json) cover 14,976 records over six seeds, with 192 separate club/seed observations each for active and practice cohorts. Active club overall spans range from 22 to 50 points; practice spans from 8 to 43. The smallest observed hairstyle counts are 11 active and 6 practice, with no duplicate complete appearance or frame recipes within these observed clubs. Independent review passes the diagnostic source/output. This verifies numerical variability, not visual distinctness, empirical world/NFL proportions, consequential career development or final managed-season acceptance. Optional null grooming values are now separated from absent fields in the report.

The [additional source review](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#joint-data-extraction-and-continuous-pigmentation--2026-10-07) identifies an actionable U.S. demographic input: the 2024 American Community Survey (ACS) Public Use Microdata Sample (PUMS). Build weighted birthplace/background cross-tabs using its original race, Hispanic-origin and ancestry fields; retain age/sex scope, allocation flags and uncertainty. These describe U.S. residents, including diaspora, rather than national birth populations or football recruitment. No extracted counts or fitted weights are currently adopted.

Implement in this order: source extraction and category crosswalk; versioned joint sampler; persistent complete appearance recipe; shared front/side/motion geometry; cohort and rendered acceptance. Reuse the existing identity, measurement and renderer modules. Keep the current simulation weights labeled assumed until reproducible extraction and calibration replace them. Check sparse cells before fitting; record a broader fallback and its sensitivity instead of inventing precision or multiplying unrelated margins. The dated NFL reporting margin constrains aggregate representation separately from country context; undisclosed identity retains a complete appearance and remains undisclosed in reports.

| Required comparison | Visible and persistent result |
| --- | --- |
| Same country/background, different individuals | Distinct compatible skin shades/undertones, iris shades, face geometry, natural hair and grooming; no complete country preset. |
| Same player, different grooming | Haircut, length, part, fade, braids/locs, facial hair, dye and gray coverage change their intended features while preserving identity, natural pigment and body. |
| Same broad eye or hair color label | Continuous shades and iris patterns remain distinguishable at dossier size; labels cannot collapse the saved variation. |
| Equal height/weight, different measured proportions | Long-legged/short-torso and short-legged/long-torso builds, different breadths and regional bulk remain compatible with total stature/mass and recorded endpoints. |
| Equal anatomy, different athletic capacities | Sprint acceleration, stride timing, jump and cutting cues change independently; bones and recorded dimensions remain fixed. |
| Tall/heavy/fast and short/light/slower fixtures | Render valid bodies and appropriate movement without imposing a single body type per position or deriving composition from speed. |
| Same player across career and transactions | Grooming and dated weight/composition can evolve; reload, trade and team-uniform changes preserve recognition and stable anatomy. |

Acceptance requires a source/assumption report for every supported country, held-out measurement residuals, cohort checks with compatible denominators, and actual roster/scouting/draft/dossier captures plus common-scale front/side/motion comparisons. Check desktop/mobile and both themes. Reject invisible eye-color differences, helmet-like hair, discontinuous shoulders/hands, contradictory torso/limb proportions and view-specific identity drift. Cosmetic changes must preserve football outcomes and simulation random streams. This contract adds required delivery work; it does not assert that the current illustrations or population fit are complete.

## Whole-player individuality — latest requirement, 2026-10-07

Treat every player as a persistent individual across portrait, full-body and moving views. Complete appearance remains a required deliverable; the existing illustrated improvements are partial. The latest clarification explicitly includes hairstyles, hair color, skin color, eye color and body frames matching measurables and proportions.

Separate controls do not imply statistically independent biological traits. [Iris pigment](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/eyecolor/) and [hair pigment](https://medlineplus.gov/genetics/understanding/traits/haircolor/) have complex, partly shared influences. Use supported conditional distributions with substantial individual variation; record any illustrative joint model as an assumption. Haircut, dye and grooming remain separate choices. Appearance must never function as a talent or potential predictor.

The final comparison set must include same-background players with visibly different hair/skin/eyes/faces; identical faces with different grooming; identical grooming with different faces; equal-height players with different mass; equal-mass players with different stature; equal-height/mass players with different compatible regional builds; and equal-frame players with different athletic motion. Include heavy-fast and light-slow players. Verify generated cohorts separately from these deliberately controlled specimens so a gallery cannot substitute for population calibration.

| Layer | Required controls and behavior | Completion evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Background | Separate birthplace, upbringing, family origins and race/ethnicity reporting; support multiple origins and diaspora. Fit dated NFL cohort representation separately from country resident context. | Same-country players can have different identities and appearances. Preserve original source categories, missing disclosure and denominators; never equate birthplace with race. |
| Hair and grooming | Cut, length, arrangement, curvature, strand thickness, density and hairline; independent natural pigment, dye and gray coverage; separate beard, moustache and brows. | Inspect shaved, fades, buzz cuts, straight/wavy/curly/coiled hair, braids, locs, longer hair and receding variants at product size. Changed grooming preserves the person underneath. |
| Complexion and eyes | Continuous skin shade and undertone; consistent exposed skin across all views. Iris shade, ring/detail and contrast remain distinct from skin/hair. | Show different complexions within the same background and readable dark/light brown, hazel, green, blue and gray eyes in expanded profiles under both themes. |
| Face | Separate forehead/brow, eye spacing/lids, nose bridge/tip/width, cheek shape, mouth/lips, chin/jaw and ears. Preserve plausible joint geometry with individual residual variation. | Same-background, same-height/weight players remain visibly distinct with identical grooming. Front and side views preserve the same landmarks; expression and blinking do not reroll geometry. |
| Body | Measured stature, mass, reach/arm and hand inputs take precedence. Preserve individual shoulder/pelvic breadth, torso/leg balance and regional girths; missing observations remain labeled estimates. | Common-scale front/side comparisons: same height/different mass, same mass/different height, and same height/mass/different compatible builds. One shared skeleton and regional volume model drive every view. |
| Athletic motion | Separate launch/acceleration, running speed, jumping and direction change. Declare whether each animation input is observed, timed or estimated; preserve recorded frame dimensions. | Same-frame players can move differently. Heavy-fast and light-slow cases remain possible; athleticism does not stretch bones or act as a body-fat measurement. |
| Continuity | Version saved natural traits, mutable grooming, dated measurements, regional composition and movement inputs separately. Reuse the existing authorities rather than generating appearance on each render. | Save/load, trade, draft-to-roster transitions and career aging preserve recognition and measurement provenance. Weight changes update regional volume without rerolling the skeleton or face. Equal-weight conditioning changes require a separate observed or explicitly estimated composition record. Verify actual roster, scouting, draft and dossier entry points. |

Research informs this design without turning national labels into cosmetic presets. The [new facial and pigmentation review](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#facial-individuality-and-pigmentation--2026-10-07) adds three sources to the evidence ledger. Regional facial controls are an implementation inference, not a fitted face-prediction model. Country-level hair/eye/skin frequencies, grooming prevalence and missing NFL physique parameters remain explicitly unresolved where compatible observations are unavailable.

Acceptance requires both a reproducible generated cohort and controlled paired fixtures. Generated cohorts establish representation and within-group variation; fixtures expose individual rendering controls and uncommon combinations. Stored diversity alone is insufficient: inspect recognizable differences at the sizes users actually see, on desktop/mobile and in both themes. No race, ethnicity, skin shade, hair or eye color may change talent, potential or football outcomes when gameplay inputs are held fixed.

The [2026 longitudinal football study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41369529/) adds evidence that total weight and regional lean composition need separate histories: its collegiate cohort showed some regional lean changes without corresponding group-level total-mass changes. This supports the record separation above as an implementation inference. It does not establish NFL aging rates or justify automatic seasonal muscle loss. The [evidence ledger](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_EVIDENCE_2026-10-06.json) now contains 30 distinct sources; unsupported population weights remain labeled assumptions.

Execute this requirement through the existing authorities: `playerDemographicModel.js` for cohort/background sampling; `playerVisualIdentity.js` for persistent natural traits and mutable grooming; `playerMeasurements.js` for dated, unit-aware body observations; `playerWorkouts.js` for separate athletic signals; and the shared portrait/body/motion renderers for visible results. Completion must include paired fixtures where changing only hair, iris, complexion, frame allocation or a movement input changes the corresponding visible channel while preserving the other saved inputs. Profile display reads must leave simulation randomness unchanged. Existing partial appearance work remains in progress until these product-level checks pass.

| Area | Implementation detail | Completion check |

Keep natural shape separate from styling, braiding and loc arrangement. | Equal cut/color with different curvature, diameter or density produces the intended visible difference. Compare loose hair separately when an arrangement conceals curvature. |
