## Complete appearance and proportional-body priority — 2026-10-07

Current access correction: actual roster/scouting/draft links now open unsigned prospects with persisted appearance and measured frames, clearly labeled scouting estimates and no hidden-rating/development disclosure. An inverse cosmetic-strength leak found by independent review was repaired and counterfactually tested. The season-review card now scrolls within the viewport so its dismissal remains reachable. Focused17 and adjacent37 tests, browser2/2, build and CANON-053 pass; [72 inspected selected-gallery/access/dialog captures](visual-qa/DEMOGRAPHIC_ACCESS_2026-10-07.json) cover desktop/mobile and both themes. Independent demographic review accepts the fictional-model L2 contract; expanded empirical calibration, final-source career drift, complete appearance and release acceptance remain open.

The requested outcome is a league of recognizable individuals: varied hairstyles, textures, hair colors, skin shades, eyes, faces and measured frames, consistently visible in roster, scouting, draft and dossier views. The following requirements consolidate the latest clarification; existing scoped renderer verification does not establish population calibration or complete delivery.

| Work package | Required implementation | Acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Background and recruiting | Preserve birthplace, upbringing, multiple family origins and dated race/ethnicity reporting separately. Use NFL cohort observations for league representation and national sources for country context. Retain diaspora, mixed identities and nondisclosure. | Separate active, practice, draft and undrafted cohort reports; compatible source denominators; missingness and uncertainty; multiple visibly distinct people with the same background. |
| Natural appearance | Persist skin shade/undertone, natural hair pigment/curvature/density, iris pigment and individual face geometry. Use overlapping distributions and individual variation; birthplace cannot select a single face or complete cosmetic preset. | Within-background channel inventories and inspected actual-size portraits; brown-eye shade variation; compatible uncommon combinations; saved traits survive trades and reloads. |
| Grooming and aging | Persist cut, length, arrangement, scalp dye, facial hair, brows, hairline and gray-fiber coverage independently. Add stable individual onset/progression parameters where modeled; keep natural pigment beneath dye. | Same person across grooming changes and career ages; partial graying remains visible with recognizable face and frame. Frequency assumptions remain explicit until suitable male athlete-age observations support fitting. |
| Measured frame | Anchor one front/side/moving skeleton to height, arm/reach, hand and available segment measurements. Reconcile current mass with regional volume, shoulder/pelvic breadth and torso/leg proportions. Missing observations retain stable estimated provenance. | Common-scale comparisons of equal height/different mass, equal mass/different height, and equal height/mass/different compatible frames; endpoint and unit checks; no bone-length changes from ratings. |
| Athletic expression | Separate speed, initial acceleration, jumping, strength and direction change. Keep game observations, timed drills and estimated motion distinguishable. Preserve heavy-fast and light-slow combinations. | Independent movement comparisons at fixed dimensions; stride/turn/jump behavior responds to the corresponding input. Athletic test results do not mechanically determine football skill, potential or draft rank. |
| Product access | Carry the saved appearance and frame through every actual profile entry point, including unsigned prospects. Scouting profiles must clearly mark estimates and protect hidden ratings/potential. | Actual roster/scouting/draft links open the same individual on desktop/mobile in both themes; profile reads preserve saved identity and random-generator state. |

Two additional primary studies inform this sequence. The [4,192-volunteer graying survey](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22716034/) supports individual aging variation, rather than a fixed age-triggered gray preset; it does not provide NFL-age probabilities. The [August 2026 Combine study](https://doi.org/10.1519/JSC.0000000000005654) separates physical performance dimensions and finds limited draft prediction. Its complete-assessment sample is not a census of NFL players and supplies no coefficients adopted here. Both are recorded in the [evidence ledger](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_EVIDENCE_2026-10-06.json); abstract review is distinguished from raw-data fitting.

Delivery order: reconcile empirical cohort definitions and assumptions → complete saved trait authority → reconcile measured geometry → bind shared renderers and movement → inspect actual product access and career continuity. Controlled rare-case fixtures supplement naturally generated cohorts without imposing per-team demographic quotas.

### Additional body and movement source requirements

The directly reviewed [CDC 2021–2023 body-measure codebook](https://wwwn.cdc.gov/Nchs/Data/Nhanes/Public/2021/DataFiles/BMX_L.htm) keeps height, mass, limb lengths and circumferences separate, with missingness, comment codes and examination weights. **Implementation inference:** a fitting pipeline must preserve those distinctions, restrict to a compatible adult cohort and report selection/weighting. These observations do not supply NFL positional frequencies or athletic ability.

The [CDC measurement manual, §§3.4.5–3.4.7](https://wwwn.cdc.gov/nchs/data/nhanes/public/2021/manuals/2021-Anthropometry-Procedures-Manual-508.pdf) measures upper arm from an acromion-region landmark to olecranon, and upper leg from inguinal crease to the proximal patella border. **Implementation inference:** neither is a renderer joint-center bone length or the existing shoulder-to-fingertip arm measure. Require endpoint metadata and a validated conversion before using either to replace saved segment proportions. Keep incompatible observations separate; preserve existing estimates until that conversion is established. No CDC data rows or fitted coefficients have been imported. Independent scoped source review accepts these two additions without findings.

Use the [Army's ANSUR II release](https://www.army.mil/article-amp/188601) to identify separate anthropometric dimensions and measurement protocols: it reports 93 measurements and 3D scans from 4,082 men and 1,986 women. Its soldier cohort cannot supply NFL or world body-shape frequencies. Before fitting covariance, inspect the actual data dictionary, restrict the analysis to the relevant cohort, reconcile endpoints/units and report validation against football measurements. No ANSUR data rows or covariance have been imported yet.

The [NFL's tracking explanation](https://operations.nfl.com/game-operations-logistics/technology/performance-tracking-data-next-gen-stats) describes position, speed and acceleration observations at 10 Hz. Use that distinction in movement provenance: a forty-yard average is not observed peak game speed; drill times, game tracking and rating-derived animation estimates need separate fields and source labels. Evaluate running, acceleration and turning against their respective inputs without changing stature, mass or permanent limb lengths. No tracking data or fitted gait model has been imported yet.

## Complete-player acceptance for the latest appearance request

The player must read as one distinct person across portraits, full-body views and animation. The [machine-readable evidence ledger](PLAYER_APPEARANCE_EVIDENCE_2026-10-06.json) binds demographic, hair, iris and body research to the work packages below. It records retrieval status, collection periods, cohort limits and permitted use; none of its source observations silently changes runtime generation weights.

- **Independent visible variation:** implement cut/length/arrangement, hair texture/density/hairline, natural pigment/dye/graying, beard/brows, continuous skin shade/undertone, iris shade and facial geometry as separately saved controls. Preserve plausible correlations and broad overlap without selecting a complete cosmetic bundle from a racial category or birthplace.
- **Proportional bodies:** measured height, weight, arm/reach and hand dimensions anchor one shared body. Shoulder/pelvic breadth, torso-to-leg ratio, limb segments and regional volume remain individual; recorded inputs take precedence and absent inputs retain estimated provenance. Height scales skeletal stature, while mass changes volume rather than uniformly stretching every body part.
- **Athletic expression:** speed, acceleration, jumping and change of direction affect their respective movement. Equal-size players may move differently; heavy-fast players remain heavy. Strength does not add unrecorded mass or alter bone lengths. Body composition is a separate observation or labeled estimate.
- **Demographic calibration:** use dated NFL player cohorts for league representation and country-specific resident sources for family/history context, preserving multiple origins and diaspora histories. World population shares do not directly become NFL recruitment weights. Nondisclosure never determines an appearance.
- **Visible completion:** inspect actual roster, scouting, draft and dossier sizes plus common-scale front/side/motion comparisons. Require same-background/different-person, same-person/different-grooming, same-size/different-frame, same-height/different-mass, same-mass/different-height and heavy-fast fixtures. Save/load, trades and aging must preserve recognition; desktop/mobile and both themes must pass.

Delivery remains ordered as evidence/cohort definitions → saved natural appearance and mutable grooming → measurement reconciliation and regional body shape → shared rendering and athletic movement → population, lifetime and actual-size visual acceptance. Existing focused improvements do not close this complete-player requirement.

### Sampling and visible acceptance required by the full-variation request

Generate each person through separately versioned background, natural appearance, grooming, measured body and movement records. Use the existing `src/domain/playerIdentity.js`, `playerDemographicModel.js`, `playerVisualIdentity.js`, `playerMeasurements.js` and `playerWorkouts.js` authorities. `leagueIdentity.js` owns the saved league fingerprint, not demographic calibration. Each fitted distribution must name its source and cohort; each unsupported conditional weight must name its simulation assumption.

1. Sample recruitment and birthplace for the selected league cohort, then family origins and upbringing with mixed and diaspora support. Fit league reporting margins separately from resident-country context. Neither a country nor a survey category selects a complete face or cosmetic preset. Changing demographic labels while preserving saved measurement/workout inputs cannot change football ability or body geometry.
2. Sample continuous natural skin shade/undertone, hair pigmentation, curvature and iris pigmentation with supported correlations and individual residual variation. Keep brow/beard pigment, scalp dye, density, hairline, graying and mutable cut/arrangement separate. The [Northern Island Melanesia study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24449225/) supports a concrete dark-skin/light-natural-hair case; it does not establish a frequency for all Pacific Islander players. Haircuts require their own dated grooming prior.
3. Resolve stature, current mass and recorded segment/breadth/girth observations before estimated anatomy. Equal height/weight must admit different compatible shoulder/pelvic breadths, torso/leg balance and regional volume. Preserve measurement endpoints and residuals. Speed, acceleration, jumping and direction change control corresponding motion; muscle definition and body fat cannot be inferred from one athleticism rating.
4. Export calibration and evaluation cohorts separately: active roster, practice squad, draft prospects and undrafted players, stratified by source year and position where evidence supports it. Report missingness, counts, quantiles and available joint relationships. Use reproducible held-out generation seeds, source-compatible uncertainty and explicit rare-category coverage; do not force every team or draft class to reproduce league percentages exactly.
5. Require actual-size roster, scouting, draft and dossier evidence plus common-scale front/side/motion comparisons. Include same-background/different-person; same-person/different-cut-or-dye; dark-skin/light-natural-hair; same-size/different-frame; same-height/different-mass; same-mass/different-height; equal-frame/different-athleticism; heavy-fast; bald/receding; and mixed-family cases. Check readable eyes and hair under both themes and on mobile. Verify saved recognition through trades, aging and reload, and reject demographic changes that alter simulation outcomes under otherwise identical inputs.

These are pending acceptance requirements. Existing stylized renderers, unique seeds and stored trait counts do not establish complete naturalistic appearance or empirically calibrated frequencies.

### Measurement-to-appearance and movement delivery contract

The latest request requires visible differences in the whole person. These mappings are implementation requirements, not fitted physiological equations. Further primary evidence and its limits are recorded in [the research expansion](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#additional-country-composition-and-athletic-measurement-research).

| Input or saved trait | Visible consequence | Required check |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Birthplace, family origins, upbringing and recruitment pathway | Plausible, overlapping histories; family context may inform supported natural pigmentation priors | Preserve local census categories and multiple identities. Same birthplace supports different ancestries and appearances; foreign-born and international-pathway status remain distinct. |
| Natural hair pigment, scalp dye, texture, length, arrangement, hairline and density | Different silhouettes, strand grouping, scalp coverage and colors; brows/beard retain separate pigment | Short/long, straight/wavy/curly/coiled, shaved/receding, braids/locs and dyed comparisons must be recognizable at actual sizes. A haircut preserves the face. |
| Skin shade/undertone and iris shade/pattern | Continuous complexion differences and readable eye colors | Include dark-skin/light-natural-hair and different complexions within the same background. Brown, hazel, green, gray and blue must be distinguishable in the actual dossier; details concealed in tiny roster eyes must be visible when expanded. |
| Height and current weight | Correct common-scale stature and total visual bulk, with a stable skeleton | Equal height/different weight changes volume; equal weight/different height changes proportions. Added mass does not uniformly enlarge heads or stretch hands. |
| Shoulder/pelvic breadth, sitting height, limb segments, reach and hand/foot dimensions | Individual shoulder-to-waist shape, torso/leg balance and limb endpoints | Recorded dimensions override compatible estimates. Hand span is not hand length; identify arm/reach endpoints. Report incompatible measurements explicitly. |
| Body composition or regional girths | Separate torso, neck, arm and leg fullness and muscle definition | Height/weight or body mass index alone cannot establish body fat. Keep observed composition distinct from stable artistic estimates; conserve the shared regional volume budget. |
| Ten-yard split/acceleration and forty-yard time/speed | Different launch response and running pace on the same frame | Lower times imply faster corresponding performance under a declared animation mapping. Heavy-fast players keep their mass. Declare one motion authority when workout results and ratings coexist. |
| Vertical/broad jump/jumping and shuttle/cone/agility | Different jump displacement and direction-change response | Keep vertical and horizontal measures distinct; shuttle/cone are combined tests, not direct joint-angle measurements. Ratings cannot alter skeletal lengths. |
| Age, grooming and dated weight/conditioning history | Gradual graying, hairline and soft-tissue changes with recognizable identity | Save/reload and trades preserve identity; historic measurements keep their date. Bench repetitions do not directly determine muscle mass or body fat. |

Country calibration proceeds country by country, with a coverage table naming geography, year, response semantics, compatible birthplace/ancestry cross-tabs and missing evidence. Brazil's self-declared color/race, South Africa's population groups and New Zealand's multiple ethnic responses cannot become one unqualified worldwide racial percentage. Resident data supplies context; league recruitment supplies athlete selection. Missing conditional country weights remain explicit assumptions until compatible observations are extracted.

Body calibration requires joint individual records where accessible: position, date, stature, mass, reach/segment measurements and workouts from the same person. Evaluate active, practice, draft and undrafted populations separately. Combine invitees and historical body-composition samples cannot establish present active-roster distributions. Report joint scatter/quantile diagnostics and missingness before fitting; retain source records and held-out seeds.

Visual acceptance must also repair the current review findings: mannequin-like facial planes, solid-cutout long hair and beards, abrupt shoulder/hand anatomy, and weak actual-size iris distinctions. Preserve measured endpoints while repairing the rendering. Unique image hashes establish differences only; inspected images establish whether those differences look like distinct natural people. These findings remain open.

Reconcile competing inputs before deriving the shared skeleton and regional shape. | Common-scale front/side checks preserve supplied dimensions and mass constraints. Compare equal height/weight with different frames, equal height/different weights and equal weight/different heights. Invalid or incompatible measurements produce an explicit reconciliation result; guessed dimensions cannot be labeled recorded. |
| Athletic expression and body history | Bind separate workout/physical inputs in `src/domain/playerWorkouts.js` to acceleration, running pace, jumping and direction change on the shared body. Store mass/conditioning changes independently from permanent skeletal proportions and facial identity. | Heavy-fast players retain bulk; equal-size players can move differently. Verify bone lengths, foot contact and pose continuity. Ratings do not fabricate body-fat measurements or new bone lengths; demographics do not modify talent, potential, personality or injury probabilities. |
| Whole-player visibility and persistence | Carry the same recipe into roster, scouting, draft, dossier, profile and animated surfaces. Preserve versioned legacy decoders and independent appearance randomness. | Inspect real generated players and controlled comparisons on desktop/mobile, both themes. Verify recognition through grooming, aging, weight changes, trades, retirement and save/reload. Population statistics, enum coverage and unique seeds cannot substitute for visibly distinct people. |

Before numerical fitting, declare the source cohort, supported joint relationships, missing-data treatment and validation split. Use compatible joint observations for body correlations; separately published ranges do not establish covariance. Where representative NFL/world pigment, grooming or anatomical distributions are unavailable, retain named simulation assumptions with broad individual overlap. Completion requires all five packages and the evidence artifacts below; scoped renderer improvements remain scoped.

### Measurement priorities and concrete comparison cases

The [March 2026 professional-football anthropometry review](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#march-2026-professional-football-anthropometry-review) adds a useful measurement inventory, not an NFL generator fit. Implement recorded breadths, sitting height and girths as separately defined observations. Biiliocristal breadth is an external pelvic-crest measurement; it cannot populate the existing hip-joint-center spacing field. Sitting height includes the head and neck; it cannot populate a torso-length field without an explicit derivation. Girth constrains a circumference, not a unique width or tissue composition.

Resolve the body in this order: normalize observation units and endpoints; reconcile competing dated observations; constrain compatible stature/reach/segment lengths; constrain observed breadths and girths; allocate remaining mass with explicit estimated composition and cross-section assumptions; then apply clothing, pose and lighting. Overconstrained or incompatible records retain the originals and expose residuals instead of silently satisfying one input by violating another. Each reconciliation records supplied value, endpoint, applied value, residual, source and fallback reason. For an estimated body, mass consistency is an artistic-model check; measured tissue fidelity requires appropriate composition evidence.

| Controlled pair or group | Vary | Preserve | Visible acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Same person, different grooming | Cut, length, arrangement, scalp dye, beard grooming | Face, natural pigmentation, eyes, skeleton and ratings | Recognizable identity with visibly different hair; scalp dye does not recolor untreated brows/beard. |
| Same background, different individuals | Face, skin undertone/shade, iris, natural hair channels and grooming within overlapping support | The selected background context | Visibly distinct people rather than one national or racial face template. |
| Equal height and weight, different builds | Compatible shoulder/pelvic breadth, torso/leg balance and regional distribution | Stature, total mass and explicitly shared measurements | Different front and side silhouettes without invented mass or stretched heads. |
| Equal frame, different athleticism | Speed, acceleration, agility and jumping, one at a time | Face, pigment, current mass and all skeletal dimensions | Corresponding pace, buildup, redirection or takeoff changes; a static pose need not imply ability. |
| Heavy-fast and light-strong examples | Actual mass, compatible dimensions and physical capabilities | Each individual's saved authority across views | Heavy-fast retains bulk; strength alone creates neither new mass nor new bone length. |
| Recorded pelvic/foot endpoints | Compatible measurements versus crest/joint mismatch and absent companions | Original source precision and metadata | Front/cut joint spacing and fitted footwear follow applicable inputs; incompatible observations explain the estimate fallback. |

Render these pairs in actual roster/scouting/draft/dossier surfaces, common-scale front/side views and movement, on desktop/mobile in both themes. Include generated cohorts alongside controlled fixtures; fixture coverage cannot establish demographic frequencies. Record implementation status separately from source calibration and inspected visibility. Population identity, cosmetic phenotype, body measurements and athletic capabilities remain separate authorities.

## Complete-player integration contract

### Recorded dimensions: current implementation boundary

Three optional imported controls now reach the canonical measurement record and shared frame: hip-joint-center spacing, barefoot heel-to-longest-toe length and maximum transverse foot width. Hip-center spacing sets frontal and lateral-cut joint separation directly; it is deliberately distinct from pelvic crest breadth. Foot dimensions set an artistic fitted shoe outline, without claiming a measured shoe allowance or tissue model. Top-level observations take precedence over stored observations; fractional precision and source metadata survive both restore seams.

Explicit incompatible endpoints, unsupported visual ranges and a lone foot dimension on a legacy player without a companion estimate retain source observations and produce a reconciliation result. The dossier exposes supplied values and explains estimate fallback. Missing fields remain unknown in the record. New generated players retain their estimated recipe, and players without supplied dimensions retain legacy silhouettes. Focused primary and independent checks pass 25/25, with 256 independent endpoint/extreme-frame probes. Browser checks pass 2/2 before and after; 16 controlled desktop/mobile dark/light gallery and dossier captures were inspected. The dossier evidence injects controlled source measurements into a real profile response; it does not establish actual imported-career browser persistence. The build passes at 280 assets / 3,201 KB. Remaining recorded shoulder/torso/segment/girth reconciliation, empirical composition, realistic whole-player rendering and final release verification remain open.

The manifest retains its 91 generated appearance/frame rows and five latent regional controls, and now records these three optional imported controls and foot-override authority with refreshed source hashes. Its seven fixture parity and seven negative-control checks pass. This refresh does not upgrade the earlier manifest review into independent full visual acceptance.

Expanded 2026-10-06 for the requested race/demographic diversity and complete variation in hairstyle, hair color, skin, eyes, frame and athletic movement. The [additional source checks](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#additional-demographic-instrument-checks-and-measurable-body-contract) add Australian response semantics, U.S. reporting-version changes and measurable-body constraints. Existing implementation evidence below remains scoped; this contract defines remaining delivery.

### Identity and appearance authority

Persist birthplace, family origins (including multiple origins), upbringing and football recruitment route separately. Preserve a dated reporting identity/projection with source instrument and unknown status. Country context informs probabilistic fictional histories; league recruitment and retained-player statistics calibrate their own cohorts. A reporting category is not a renderer preset. Require visually different individuals within every supported background and preserve mixed/diaspora histories.

Create a code-derived trait manifest with one row per control: saved field and recipe version, natural or mutable status, source/assumption, decoder, valid range/combinations, front/side/motion consumers, concealed states and actual-size acceptance evidence. Include all of the following:

The initial [current-source manifest](PLAYER_TRAIT_MANIFEST_2026-10-06.json) now supplies 91 generated DTO-field rows and five latent regional controls. It records authority/units, source hashes, a reproducible render fixture and conditional/downstream applicability. Seven baseline fixture parity checks and seven unused-field negative controls pass. This is a structural inventory; complete valid-range/combination coverage, actual-size inspected evidence per advertised channel and demographic/composition calibration remain required. The isolated frame probes deliberately do not claim coherent physical counterfactuals.

| Trait group | Required independent controls | Integration requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Scalp hair | Natural pigment, curl/coil curvature, strand width, density, hairline; cut, length, part/arrangement, dye, partial gray | Same person can change cut/dye without changing natural pigment, face or body. Keep texture distinct from hairstyle. |
| Facial hair and brows | Natural pigment, thickness/density, beard pattern, length and grooming; separate dye where supported | A shaved scalp still retains individual brows/beard. Scalp dye does not automatically dye them. |
| Skin | Continuous shade, undertone and bounded individual detail | Shared face/body palette and consistent lighting; dark and light complexions retain readable facial structure in both UI themes. |
| Eyes and face | Iris shade/pattern, eye shape/spacing/lids, brows, nose, upper/lower lips, cheeks, jaw/chin, forehead and ears | Recognizable saved landmarks across front/profile/motion; iris differences visible at dossier scale. No fixed national face template. |
| Body | Stature, mass, reach, shoulders/pelvis, torso/leg balance, limb segments, hands/feet, chest depth and regional girths | One shared skeleton and shape authority for portraits, side view, movement and uniform/pads. Distinguish observations from estimates. |

### Measurement and athleticism rules

Store units, endpoint definition, provenance and observation time for every imported dimension. Distinguish hand span from hand length, shoulder-to-fingertip reach from bone segment length, and sitting height from torso length. Convert units once at the input boundary. Conflicting measurements require a documented reconciliation result; absent dimensions use stable estimates with provenance. Preserve original measurements when an artistic rendering bound is exceeded and expose the discrepancy in verification rather than silently claiming fidelity.

Height controls common-scale stature and skeleton constraints; current weight controls a declared mass/volume budget. Skeletal breadth and soft tissue remain separate. Equal height/weight must permit different plausible chest, waist, hip and limb distributions. Position-specific football observations may guide composition when compatible joint evidence is available; a position label is not one fixed body. Conditioning and weight updates preserve bone lengths and facial identity, with dated history for mutable body state.

Athleticism controls the corresponding action: speed governs travel pace, acceleration governs buildup, jumping governs takeoff/flight and agility governs braking/redirection. Keep the heavy-fast athlete heavy and allow equal-size athletes to move differently. Strength alone does not create unrecorded mass, and an overall rating does not estimate body fat. Demographic/cosmetic fields have no direct effect on performance, potential, personality or injury risk.

### Acceptance and calibration artifacts

Deliver the trait manifest, a source-compatible demographic/cohort report, a measurement reconciliation report and inspected actual-player captures. Compare active/practice players, draft/undrafted entrants and career survivors separately, using multiple seeds and uncertainty appropriate to each source. Retain source year, geography, denominator, multiple-response semantics, missingness and assumption status; small team rosters need not reproduce league percentages exactly.

The controlled visual set must include same background/different people; same face/different cut, texture and pigment; same complexion/different face; same face/different eyes; same height/different weight; same weight/different height; equal height/weight/different frame; equal frame/different athleticism; and short-heavy, tall-light, tall-heavy, compact-muscular and heavy-fast cases. Inspect roster, scouting, draft and dossier views at real sizes, plus common-scale front/side/motion comparisons on desktop/mobile in both themes. Numerical differences and enum coverage alone do not satisfy visibility.

Verify each identity through grooming, aging, mass changes, trades, retirement and save/reload. Legacy saves retain their decoder unless an explicit migration is applied. Appearance generation uses independent deterministic streams and cannot consume gameplay randomness. Unsupported demographic, hairstyle, pigment or anatomy coefficients remain named simulation assumptions until suitable evidence supports a replacement.

## Full-player requirement: delivery sequence and acceptance contract

Updated 2026-10-06 for the explicit request for different hairstyles, hair colors, skin colors, eye colors and bodies matching measurables and athleticism. This section orders the remaining work below; existing foundations are not equivalent to completed appearance delivery. The [research refresh](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#current-research-check-country-response-semantics-and-delivery-order) distinguishes reviewed observations from proposed simulation choices.

1. **Freeze evidence and model definitions.** Record country/territory, observation year, resident or football cohort, sampling frame, category definitions, response multiplicity, denominator and retrieval status for each adopted source. Use exact TIDES counts for the dated league margin; keep nondisclosure. Keep birthplace, family origins, upbringing and recruitment route separate. Fit neither a worldwide race table nor national phenotype frequencies from incompatible census categories. Preserve existing fictional recruitment assumptions until suitable observations replace them.
2. **Complete the saved individual recipe.** Produce a code-derived manifest covering natural hair pigment, curl/coil shape, strand width, density and hairline; hairstyle, length, part, dye and partial gray; beard/brows; skin shade/undertone; iris pigment/pattern; facial landmarks and individual detail. Partial silver fibers, versioned hair arrangement, within-head fiber variation and finer independent facial controls now have scoped rendering evidence; realistic overall rendering and whole-player visual acceptance remain required. Retain older decoders. Every channel needs saved authority, applicability rules and front/side/motion support or a documented concealed state. Keep family-history sampling probabilistic with individual residual variation; no birthplace selects one fixed face or cosmetic bundle.
3. **Unify anatomy before polishing animation.** Canonical height, weight and compatible measured reach/limb endpoints constrain one skeleton used by every view. New-player recipes now provide stable estimated pelvic breadth, independent upper/lower limb ratios and foot dimensions, plus separate artistic regional allocation, waist and chest-depth controls. Complete compatible recorded-dimension overrides and provenance before claiming measured fidelity for those extra dimensions. Fit regional chest/abdomen/hip/arm/leg shape separately from skeletal lengths. Declare density and mass-allocation assumptions. Do not translate body mass index, strength or overall rating into a claimed body-fat percentage. Weight/conditioning updates preserve face and skeletal identity.
4. **Render meaningful differences at actual sizes.** Finish each channel on shared portrait, side and moving surfaces, then inspect roster thumbnails, scouting, draft and dossiers. Hair geometry must distinguish cut from texture; scalp dye must preserve natural beard/brow color unless separately changed. Skin must remain consistent across face/body under shared lighting. Iris differences must be readable in dossier close-ups. Uniforms/pads overlay the same body, and common-scale comparisons must reveal stature and frame instead of automatic zoom hiding them.
5. **Express athleticism through the same body.** Speed, acceleration, jumping and agility each drive their corresponding movement cues. Equal-size athletes can move differently; a heavy fast athlete retains bulk. Preserve bone lengths, joint limits, grounded contact and loop continuity. Artistic timing remains labeled until calibrated to compatible workout/motion evidence. Cosmetic identity has no direct effect on ratings, potential, injury risk or personality.
6. **Verify populations, combinations and lifetime recognition.** Use multi-seed active/practice, draft/undrafted and retained-career cohorts; compare source-compatible margins and joint distributions, reporting uncertainty and assumptions. Check identity through trades, aging, grooming, conditioning and save restoration without consuming gameplay randomness. A statistical diversity count cannot close visual acceptance.

### Concrete calibration and renderer bindings

The complete person is the acceptance unit. Hair, skin and eyes must be distinguishable on the actual player surfaces, and the measured body must be recognizable across every pose. Use these bindings when implementing the six steps above:

The implementation manifest must enumerate concrete controls rather than one generic diversity seed:

- **Hair:** cut, arrangement/part placement, length, texture/curvature, strand thickness and within-head variation, density, hairline, natural pigment, dye and individual gray fibers. Include brows and facial hair with their own grooming/color state.
- **Face and pigment:** skin shade and undertone, iris pigment/ring/pattern, eye spacing and eyelids, brow shape, nose bridge/width/projection, separate upper/lower lip shape, cheeks, jaw, chin, ears and restrained freckles/moles. Keep the same face across all views and grooming changes.
- **Frame:** stature, mass, measured reach/arm/hand endpoints, shoulder and pelvic breadth, torso/leg balance, upper/lower arm and leg ratios, hand length versus span, foot dimensions, chest depth and regional arm/abdomen/hip/thigh/calf volume. Distinguish measured inputs from stable estimates.
- **Movement:** separate speed, acceleration, jumping and direction-change controls on that same skeleton; preserve body size and individual identity. Changing a speed rating alone must not slim a player, and changing strength alone must not lengthen bones or add unrecorded mass.

For every control, the manifest records saved field/decoder, derivation and provenance, valid combinations, front/side/motion consumers and actual-size visibility. Supported-but-concealed states remain explicit. Finer facial controls and independent artistic regional shape now have scoped implementation evidence. Next improve whole-person lighting, contours and movement against actual inspected portraits, complete measured-dimension handling and calibrate compatible anatomical/composition observations; new enum values alone do not close those gaps.

### Generation and calibration decisions

Treat the requested diversity as individual variation throughout the population, with the following implementation order:

1. Build a source-compatible joint recruitment model for birthplace, family origins and upbringing, then calibrate its dated league reporting projection. National resident data informs country context, while football entrants require separate pathway evidence. League totals alone cannot identify country-by-identity probabilities. Until joint observations are available, export each chosen conditional probability as a versioned simulation assumption, including the current 4% international-birth prior and 75% local-family preference.
2. Generate a stable natural appearance recipe with overlapping pigmentation and facial variation within every background. Hair pigment, texture/curvature, fiber width and density are distinct channels; skin shade/undertone and iris pigment/detail are distinct channels. Preserve mixed-family histories and unknown reporting identity. Unknown identity still gets a fully specified individual appearance.
3. Apply independently saved grooming: cut, compatible length and part, dye, beard and brows. Styling may respond probabilistically to age and upbringing with individual choice; the reviewed studies supply no representative country/NFL haircut frequencies. Do not present artistic styling priors as observed demographic statistics.
4. Constrain the shared skeleton to recorded height, reach and compatible arm/hand endpoints. Estimate absent pelvis, segment and foot dimensions with explicit provenance. Allocate current mass over coherent chest, waist, hips and limbs independently of permanent face and bone lengths. Sample multiple plausible builds at equal height/mass; use reviewed position-specific joint evidence before claiming fitted anatomy.
5. Connect physical capabilities to their corresponding movement. Preserve a heavy-fast athlete's bulk, a tall athlete's stature and an individual's limb proportions. Check equal-frame athletes with contrasting speed, acceleration, agility and jumping separately.

Calibration must report source counts, missingness and uncertainty for active/practice players, entrants and retained careers separately. Match supported marginals without imposing per-team identity quotas. Do not force every small roster to reproduce rounded league percentages or relabel overlapping national census responses as mutually exclusive global races. Names, appearance and athletic ability remain independently reviewable outputs.

### Latest hair-arrangement evidence and remaining limits

New players save `hair-arrangement-v1`; an explicit part edit uses `player-grooming-v2`. Part placement is visible on applicable side-part, swept and long styles. Independently seeded fiber width/shape variation reaches front, side and moving views, while shaved hair and structured braids/locs explicitly conceal inapplicable loose-fiber controls. Existing identities retain the old decoder unless intentionally edited.

Corrected contract tests pass 4/4; actual gallery/dossier browser tests pass 2/2 with save/reload continuity. The legacy comparison passes 1,008 front/side render comparisons, and the build passes. Independent scoped review passes. These results establish artistic control and persistence, not demographic frequency calibration or complete naturalism. The actual desktop dossier captures revealed excessive vertical stretching in profile information. Content alignment now keeps the nameplate, badges and information compact while expanded body studies retain their size; matched before/after browser checks each pass 1/1, with desktop/mobile dark/light captures inspected and independent scoped review passing. Whole-player and whole-app visual acceptance remain open.

### Saved individual anatomical proportions

`individual-anatomy-v1` gives new players independently seeded upper-arm/forearm and thigh/shin shares, pelvic breadth and foot length/width. Shares divide the existing total bone lengths rather than changing recorded reach or stature. Static limb contours and articulated surfaces use the same joint-relative profile, and limb diameters are solved against the existing artistic regional volume budget. Pelvic breadth changes frontal hip attachment spacing; side views conceal that transverse breadth. Shoes share dimension-dependent front/side silhouettes and the existing ankle-to-floor height.

These are persistent artistic estimates, independent of demographics, overall, potential and physical ratings. Conditioning/mass changes retain those skeletal proportions. Older players retain the original decoder. The recipe survives save restoration and rejects unknown versions/extra fields. A shared rendering boundary makes optional-field bounds consistent between static geometry, inverse kinematics and motion surfaces; malformed values retain legacy estimates without changing the source record.

Focused repaired checks pass 33/33, including independent numerical limb-volume integration, exact bone lengths/contact over all four movement modes, save/RNG continuity, malformed DTOs and 252 preserved legacy render comparisons. The controlled gallery compares old renderers against new renderers on the same new-model fixture DTOs; it is a renderer comparison, not a complete old-generation population baseline. Actual generated dossier/restore browser checks pass 2/2; 20 desktop/mobile dark/light gallery and actual-player images were inspected. Independent round two passes 12/12 and all original malformed-DTO reproductions. The build passes at 277 assets / 3,180 KB. Extra imported measurement endpoints, fitted anatomical covariance, independent regional composition and full naturalism remain required.

| Authority | Required binding | Evidence before adopting coefficients |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Birthplace, family origins, upbringing and recruitment | Sample overlapping individual histories; retain local demographic definitions and a separately dated NFL reporting projection. Names and styling use family/upbringing context with individual choice. | Source-compatible cohort exports, mixed/diaspora cases and explicit unknowns. Do not fit country-by-race cells from league totals alone; multiple joint distributions can share the same margins. |
| Natural pigment and facial structure | Stable skin/undertone, iris, hair pigment/texture and facial landmarks; separate mutable cut, part, dye, beard and grooming. Same background must yield many visibly different people. | Matched actual-size renders plus distributions within and across backgrounds. Hair curvature and diameter remain separate; no single ancestry palette or style preset supplies the entire appearance. |
| Recorded stature, mass and reach | Constrain a single skeleton and shared body mesh/geometry, including shoulder/hip breadth, torso/leg balance, individual segment ratios, hands and feet. | Preserve measurement endpoints and precision; common-scale front/side/pose checks. Label missing dimensions estimated and retain a documented policy for measurements outside rendering bounds. |
| Composition and regional allocation | Distinguish skeletal breadth, lean/fat estimates, chest depth, waist/hip and limb girths; equal height/mass admits different plausible frames. | Compatible positional joint records and held-out checks where obtainable. The reviewed 1,958-prospect composition study does not support deriving body fat from BMI; the historical 411-player DXA study does not provide a current universal anatomical covariance model. Both sources are linked in the research record. |
| Individual physical performance | Use recorded workout results when compatible and game physical ratings otherwise: acceleration, running, jumping and direction change get separate mappings. Strength may affect bounded definition without changing measured mass. | Document event, units, missing tests and measurement versus artistic mapping. Sprint time alone cannot identify top speed, stride length or cadence; jump reach is not jump displacement. Keep a heavy fast player heavy and preserve limb lengths throughout motion. |

Before tuning, record which parameters are measured, fitted, estimated or artistic, and declare source-appropriate tolerances. Completion requires the channel matrix, cohort report, anatomical checks, actual-size inspected captures and save/career continuity; unsupported world/NFL hairstyle and pigment frequencies remain explicit assumptions.

### Saved finer facial features: implementation and visual boundary

Shared facial lighting now follows the fine-face landmarks on front, profile and moving heads. Softer nose/lip contours and cheek/orbit shading preserve base pigmentation and saved skull dimensions; exterior beard renders above the skin lighting. The corrected implementation passes 20 focused checks, including 48 legacy SVG and 30 skull-contour comparisons, both actual browser checks and the Pages build. Twenty-four desktop/mobile dark/light before/after gallery, portrait and movement captures were inspected; independent scoped review passes. The artwork remains stylized and small moving-head differences remain subtle. This closes the scoped lighting correction, not full naturalism or complete mixed-trait acceptance. The earlier full core run finished 432/432 before these lighting edits and is historical evidence; current-source broad verification remains required.

New players save `fine-face-v1` with independent nasal root/wing width, upper/lower lip fullness, chin projection, forehead slope and brow ridge controls. Front and moving frontal heads use the shared nose/lip geometry and brow-plane shading; side and moving profile heads use the shared lip, chin, forehead and brow contour. Sagittal chin/forehead controls are concealed in the frontal view, and transverse nasal widths are concealed in profile. Styling and career identity changes preserve the structural recipe. Older players retain their renderer unless an explicit fine recipe is supplied.

Focused checks pass 22/22, including 48 exact legacy SVG comparisons, save/grooming continuity, malformed presentation controls and complete SVG resources for v2/v3 imports with or without natural detail. Independent review found a missing gradient on valid legacy imports; it is fixed and second-round review passes. Actual generated dossier/reload checks pass 2/2, with portrait/body/side/movement captures on desktop/mobile in both themes. The delivered contours remain artistic projections; finer controls and passed persistence checks do not establish realistic overall visual quality. Actual portraits are still stylized, small mobile differences are subtle, and complete mixed-trait/demographic cohorts plus realistic face/body lighting and movement acceptance remain open.

### Saved regional shape: implementation and calibration boundary

New players save `regional-body-shape-v1` independently of their face and skeleton. Separate trunk, arm and neck allocations share one volume budget; the legs receive its residual. Waist taper and nominal chest depth vary independently. This gives equal-height/equal-weight athletes distinct front, side and moving silhouettes while preserving recorded mass and bone lengths. Older saves retain the previous decoder. Strength changes surface definition; demographic identity and overall grade do not supply body-fat estimates.

Focused checks pass 31/31, including 18 exact legacy-frame comparisons, save continuity, independent numerical torso integration and all four movement modes. Actual generated dossier/save-reload browser checks pass 2/2. These establish artistic control and persistence, not a fitted NFL composition distribution. External pelvic/glute shape, compatible measured-dimension overrides, composition/conditioning history, full facial visual quality and whole-player visual acceptance remain required. Differences must remain readable at actual dossier and thumbnail sizes; a small numerical change alone cannot close a trait row.

The [July 2026 longitudinal college study](https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/nutrition/articles/10.3389/fnut.2026.1901586/full) supplies additional context for separating mass and composition over time. Its small, single-program Division II cohort and total-body measurements cannot calibrate these regional controls or a universal NFL growth curve. Follow the source limits in the research record before replacing artistic coefficients.

### Required fixture groups and exit evidence

| Group | Controlled cases | Required result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pigmentation and face | Same face across supported skin/undertone and iris states; same pigment with different facial structure; distinct people sharing birthplace/family origins; mixed-family histories | Visible individual differences, continuous overlapping variation, consistent face/body coloration; no national face template |
| Hair and grooming | Every supported cut with applicable textures; same cut with different natural colors and dye; hairline/density/length extremes; partial gray; shaved scalp with beard; beard/brows independently varied | Distinct readable geometry/color; concealed channels explicitly recorded; no reroll of face or natural traits |
| Frame and mass | Same height/different weight; same weight/different height; same height/weight with different breadth, torso/leg balance and regional allocation; short-heavy, tall-light, tall-heavy, compact-muscular | Measurement landmarks match canonical inputs under common scale; front/side agree; plausible distinct silhouettes without changing recorded values |
| Athletic motion | Equal frame with low/high speed, acceleration, agility and jump separately; heavy-fast and light-slow combinations | Appropriate movement differences while the same mass, skeleton, face and grooming persist; continuous contact and articulation |
| Career continuity | Trade, age, dye/cut changes, explicit mass changes, retirement and restore; legacy and incomplete imports | Stable recognizable person, unchanged gameplay random streams and explicit measurement/recipe provenance |

For each group, keep numerical checks and inspected desktop/mobile dark/light captures at the actual touched surfaces. Close the work only with a generated-to-rendered coverage matrix, source/cohort report, measurement/composition report and career continuity evidence. All unsupported probabilities stay marked as design assumptions. This update changes requirements and sequencing, not runtime generation weights.

## Required outcome: distinct complete athletes with measured proportions

Use the [source-grounded trait coverage inventory](PLAYER_TRAIT_COVERAGE.md) to turn the full appearance requirement into reviewable work. It identifies existing channels and missing coverage, including partial-gray strands, hair part, finer facial geometry, pelvic/segment/foot dimensions and independent regional composition. Seeded fields or supported enums do not establish visible acceptance. Preserve the dated demographic evidence and label current complexion/pigmentation/physique probabilities as artistic until compatible cohort calibration passes.

The latest user requirement covers the entire player: different races/backgrounds, hairstyles, natural/dyed/gray hair, skin shades/undertones, eye colors, facial features and coherent bodies matching height, weight, proportions and athleticism. These are mandatory acceptance requirements. [The refined evidence](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#complete-appearance-and-frame-refined-evidence-and-delivery-requirements) adds professional breadth/girth/length distinctions and seasonal-composition evidence. Research and existing stylized foundations do not establish completed delivery.

### Required visual comparisons and completion artifacts

Use controlled pairs under identical lighting, camera scale and pose: same face/different hair; same cut/different texture and color; same complexion/different face; same face/different iris; same height/different mass; same mass/different height; same height/mass/different frame; same frame/different athleticism. Follow isolated pairs with mixed combinations and actual roster, scouting, draft and dossier surfaces on desktop/mobile in both themes. Tiny iris differences or hidden texture cannot pass solely because saved values differ.

Deliver four artifacts: a source/provenance and cohort report; a generated-to-rendered trait coverage matrix; a measurement/composition fit report distinguishing observations from assumptions; and an inspected image set plus career/save-continuity results. Declare statistical tolerances from source precision and sample limits before tuning. Demographic realism, anatomical plausibility and visible individual diversity each need their own passing evidence. No runtime probabilities or saved appearances change through this plan update.

## Additional requirement: complete individual appearance and physical diversity

The [additional position-specific research](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#position-specific-physical-diversity-additional-october-review) informs the next implementation pass. Treat hair style/color/texture, skin, eyes, face, frame, proportions and individual movement as mandatory visible channels. Existing implementation and earlier scoped checks do not close this requirement.

Implementation handoff:

1. **Evidence and population model:** reconcile dated NFL player categories, country resident context and football recruitment pathways. Preserve birthplace, family origins, upbringing and reporting identity independently. Validate league/cohort distributions across seeds, with overlap and mixed backgrounds; do not infer individual race from birthplace or use demographic fields to assign ability.
2. **Saved trait inventory:** map every supported trait to its existing generator field, saved recipe/version, front/side/motion renderer and inspected fixture. Reuse existing fields; add missing fields with explicit migration and estimate/source status. Keep natural pigment separate from hairstyle, dye and age-related changes.
3. **Measured frame:** fit compatible position-level joint measurements when available, retaining fractional precision, missingness and event provenance. Height/reach constrain skeletal endpoints; mass constrains regional volume. Shoulder/hip breadth, torso/leg share, chest/waist depth, arms, thighs, calves, hands and feet vary coherently. Unmeasured composition and dimensions remain estimates with declared assumptions.
4. **Athletic expression:** map speed, acceleration, agility, jump and strength to their respective motion/definition channels with documented units or artistic mappings. Do not collapse them into one appearance slider. Complete cutting movement; preserve segment lengths, mass, foot contact and individual identity through every pose.
5. **Acceptance:** inspect real dossiers, roster/draft thumbnails and body studies on desktop/mobile in both themes. Compare isolated traits and interactions: same country/different heritage, mixed families, same skin/different face, different eyes on deep/light skin, natural hair versus dye/gray, equal height/weight/different frame, equal frame/different athleticism, heavy-fast, short-heavy and tall-light players. Verify trade, aging, grooming and save continuity. Stored diversity, enum coverage and SVG differences cannot substitute for visible differences.

Deliver a trait coverage matrix, demographic/cohort report, measurement-fit report and inspected image set before marking the whole-player requirement complete. Numerical fit tolerances must be declared from the reviewed data and measurement precision before tuning; unsupported coefficients remain simulation assumptions. This plan update changes no runtime probabilities, player saves or rendering behavior.

## Latest user requirement: every visible attribute and measured body frame

The [additional hair morphology review](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#whole-player-follow-up-hair-morphology-and-measurable-rendering) extends the whole-athlete work. | Input or saved channel | Required visible result | Acceptance comparison |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Hair cut, length, curl/coil, strand width, density and hairline | Individually varied silhouettes and readable texture, including compatible fades, straight/wavy hair, curls/coils, braids, locs, long hair and bald/receding variants | Same saved face with every legal style/texture pair; inspect at actual profile and thumbnail sizes; suppressed controls are documented |
| Natural hair pigment, dye, gray; independent beard/brow grooming | Distinct scalp colors and age cues with coherent natural facial hair and explicit dye overrides | Matched natural/dyed/gray cases, including light/dark skin and sparse hair; stable underlying identity after save and transfer |
| Skin shade/undertone, iris color, eye shape and facial landmarks | Recognizably different people within the same birthplace, family background and position | Readable iris/skin contrast in both themes; face diversity without nationality-specific templates |
| Height, arm/reach and available recorded limb dimensions | Correct stature, skeletal endpoints and individual torso/leg proportions | Shared camera scale; preserve recorded precision and measurement landmarks; label missing dimensions as estimates |
| Weight, shoulder/hip frame and estimated regional composition | Coherent chest, waist, neck, upper arms, forearms, thighs and calves rather than uniform width scaling | Same height/different mass; same mass/different height; same height/mass/different frame; compact-heavy and tall-light extremes |
| Strength and power | Bounded muscular definition consistent with mass and estimated composition | Equal measurements/different strength, without inventing a measured body-fat percentage |
| Speed, acceleration, jumping and agility | Distinct cadence, stride, launch, jump and direction-change behavior while preserving the saved skeleton and body size | Heavy-fast/light-slow counterexamples; fixed measurements with changed physical traits; reduced-motion equivalent |
| Aging and changing current mass | Stable facial/skeletal identity with bounded aging, grooming and regional-volume updates | Career checkpoints, trade/save continuity and matched before/after views; no annual face re-roll |

Sequence: finish source/provenance and cohort exports → persist a versioned individual recipe → calibrate correlated measurements and estimated composition → implement all visible channels → connect movement to physical traits → inspect controlled comparisons and actual player surfaces → verify career and population continuity. World population shares cannot substitute for NFL recruitment probabilities.

Before numerical tuning, declare within-position measurement tolerances, source sample sizes/missingness and artistic assumptions. Check skin consistency, eye/hair readability, grooming/age continuity, reduced motion, save/load and long-term recognition. Source-level uniqueness or different SVG hashes alone cannot pass. |

Finish these packages before claiming the user's complete appearance requirement is delivered.

## Whole-athlete requirement: explicit delivery and evidence

The clarified requirement covers the entire visible athlete: hairstyle, texture, natural/dyed/gray hair color, skin shade and undertone, eye color and iris detail, facial geometry, facial hair, skeletal frame, torso/leg balance, regional mass and movement. Existing stylized portraits and saved trait variety are foundations; the complete appearance overhaul remains open.

[Additional reviewed pigmentation and measurement evidence](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#whole-athlete-clarification-pigmentation-and-measurement-endpoints) adds these concrete tasks to the work packages below:

1. **Field-to-pixel inventory.** Map every supported appearance trait to its generator, saved recipe/version, renderer and actual dossier-size fixture. Distinguish natural pigment from dye/graying and texture from hairstyle. Record missing or visually ineffective channels. Use controlled pairs plus combinations across skin shades; preserve within-country and mixed-family variation. Adopt correlated appearance sampling only where justified, with individual residual variation and documented artistic fallbacks.
2. **Measurement-to-body audit.** Store method, endpoints, units and precision with newly supported dimensions. Combine hand span must not become longitudinal hand length; fingertip-ended arm length must not gain an extra hand. Resolve compatible height/reach/mass against a common-scale skeleton before allocating regional volume. Keep conflicting observations and estimate provenance. Same height/weight can still have different shoulder breadth, torso/leg balance and mass distribution. No racial or nationality template determines athletic anatomy.
3. **Whole-athlete visual acceptance.** Inspect front, side and articulated motion views at one camera scale, then actual dossiers on desktop/mobile in both themes. Include short-heavy, tall-light, tall-heavy, equal-measurement/different-frame and heavy-fast athletes. Athleticism must visibly influence stride, acceleration, direction changes and jumping without silently changing height, weight or reach. Hair/eye/skin combinations remain legible, joint and ground contact coherent, and grooming/aging/save restoration preserve recognition. Reduced-motion support remains required.

Population checks separately cover resident/world context, dated NFL representation and recruited player cohorts. Volunteer pigmentation samples cannot supply national or NFL frequency estimates without a justified sampling/recruitment model. No new demographic probabilities or rendering completion claim is made by this plan update. Final implementation, current-source full tests, managed-career proof and canonical visual/release evidence remain required.

## Expanded full-player implementation specification

The player's whole appearance is required scope: visibly different hairstyles, natural/dyed/gray hair colors, skin shades and undertones, eye colors and details, individual faces, and bodies whose scale and proportions match canonical measurables. [The additional research and calibration contract](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#expanded-calibration-requirements-demographics-appearance-and-measured-proportions) distinguish reviewed evidence from candidates and artistic assumptions. Current appearance foundations do not close this work.

| Work package | Concrete implementation requirement | Acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Demographic calibration | Extend the existing country evidence inventory with versioned source schemes, extraction locations, cohort denominators and recruitment assumptions. Keep birth, upbringing, family histories, citizenship and NFL reporting category distinct. Preserve multi-origin and diaspora families within the same birthplace. | Reports for league category, foreign birth and supported country/family relationships; uncertainty and missing disclosure retained. Resident/world shares are not treated as NFL entrant shares. Existing numeric affinities remain fictional until supported calibration replaces them. |
| Independent natural and mutable appearance | Inventory existing saved fields before adding channels. Cover complexion/undertone; iris shade/pattern; hair pigment, curvature, strand thickness, density, hairline and length; facial landmarks, brows and facial hair. Separate dye, cut, beard grooming, gray coverage and optional personal markings from enduring identity. | Each control maps to a saved version, renderer and visibly inspected dossier fixture. Natural and dyed hair remain distinguishable; shaved hair retains its natural trait record. Same-country players show multiple faces and cosmetic combinations. Unsupported styling frequencies are labeled artistic. |
| Measurement semantics and skeleton | Add explicit endpoint/method provenance to newly supported dimensions. Separate hand span from hand length, skeletal breadth from outer shoulder width, and arm length from upper-arm/forearm segments. Preserve recorded values and pose definitions; review the current reach-minus-two-arms shoulder estimate before treating it as anatomy. | Fractional measured values survive import/save/display. Conflicting measurements are retained with provenance and a documented visual fallback. Common-scale height and reach endpoints agree with canonical data. Legacy visual recipes remain available. |
| Regional physique | Replace artistic regional fractions only after reviewing compatible cohort tables. Use correlated frame, torso/leg balance, shoulder/hip breadth, chest/abdomen depth, neck and tapered limbs. Separate skeleton, soft tissue, clothing/pads and definition. | Equal height/different mass; equal mass/different height; equal height/mass/different frame or reach. Unpadded front and side views show the differences without zoom masking them. Volume estimates are not presented as measured fat or muscle percentages. |
| Athletic movement | Map physical capacities to bounded cadence, stride, acceleration, change of direction, jumping and posture while respecting limb geometry. Keep technique/awareness/overall rating out of body sculpting. | Equal-size/different-athleticism fixtures visibly differ in movement while preserving dimensions; heavy-fast athletes remain heavy. Ground contact and joint articulation work across frames; reduced motion remains supported. |
| Lifetime and population verification | Freeze enduring identity and version changed sampling; grooming and age layers may evolve. Reuse existing save, measurement, portrait and workout suites. Track initial active/practice, entrants and survivors separately. | Controlled cosmetic/body galleries plus generated cohorts across seeds; all supported combinations reviewed at dossier size; save/load, trades, aging and the required managed career preserve recognition. Demographic changes cannot alter talent, development, injury or valuation. |

Implementation order: complete the field/source inventory → extract reviewed joint calibration data and declare fallbacks → version changed generation → validate measurement semantics → solve skeletal and regional body proportions → refine face/hair and athletic movement → inspect controlled and naturally generated athletes → verify career continuity. Definition of done requires both credible population distributions and visibly convincing athletes. Statistical diversity alone and different image hashes cannot satisfy the visual requirement.

## Required next appearance work: demographic calibration and the whole athlete

The complete-appearance requirement includes both the generated population and the visible result. [Additional primary research](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#full-appearance-and-athletic-proportions--research-follow-through) covers worldwide hair morphology, regional NFL abdominal composition and a 2026 MRI/sprint study. These support separate trait and regional-body controls; they do not establish exact country-conditioned frequencies or a formula from athletic ratings to physique.

The latest user clarification requires visibly different hairstyles, hair colors, skin colors, eye colors and individual faces, together with bodies that communicate canonical height, weight, proportions and athletic movement. This is required implementation scope. Existing recipe fields and distinct image hashes alone do not satisfy it. [The expanded research](PLAYER_IDENTITY_RESEARCH_2026-10-06.md#birthplace-family-diversity-and-measurement-methods--additional-review) records newly reviewed Canadian joint demographics, Australian ancestry-question versions and body-composition measurement limits.

1. **Calibrate the population model before changing weights.** Keep NFL reporting-category observations, birth country, upbringing, each parent's origins and detailed family histories separate. Use the dated TIDES player table for a league-level reference; use national joint tables for relevant resident context. Document the recruitment adjustment between residents and football entrants. Export denominators, collection year, age/position coverage, missingness, mapping and uncertainty. The current 4% international-birth share and country affinities are fictional. Fitting the NFL margin does not validate those country conditionals. Review all 47 supported locations and retain explicit assumptions where matching evidence is missing.
2. **Replace unverified appearance dependencies only through a new recipe version.** Audit complexion-band sampling and the current `weightedPalette` light-color multiplier `1 - .55 * complexion`. It is an artistic coupling, not measured country/family-conditioned hair or iris evidence. Record which dependencies have matching observations and which are creative; retain overlapping within-background variation. Preserve older saved decoders and isolate identity/grooming randomness from football simulation. Do not infer a real player's race from a photo or name, or use race to alter ability, growth or injury risk.
3. **Complete visible face and grooming coverage.** Independently expose natural hair pigment, dye, gray coverage, texture/curvature, length, density, hairline, cut, brow/beard pigment and grooming; continuous complexion/undertone; iris shade/pattern, eye/lid shape and stable face proportions. Natural trait correlations can use documented joint evidence; haircut and dye remain mutable personal choices. Generate local, diaspora and mixed-family examples within the same birthplace. Derive the coverage manifest from supported renderer controls and inspect their actual visual effects, including mobile brown/hazel distinction and light/dark hair visibility.
4. **Solve the measured body before styling it.** Canonical stature, mass, arm/reach/hand measurements and landmarks drive a stable skeleton and regional volume. Separately model shoulder/hip frame, torso/leg balance, chest/waist depth, neck, upper arm/forearm, thighs and calves. Fit plausible joint ranges using matching NFL/college/entry cohorts, retaining collection methods; do not pool composition-device estimates as equivalent measurements. Unrecorded frame and composition remain estimates. Strength can influence bounded definition and posture; speed/agility/jumping inform movement. Neither demographic identity nor overall rating selects a body. Heavy-fast athletes retain their mass.
5. **Prove the combined result.** Inspect common-scale front and side/three-quarter comparisons: equal height/different weight, equal weight/different height, equal height/weight/different reach or frame, and equal measurements/different strength or speed. Include compact-heavy, tall-heavy, tall-light and intermediate athletes in every applicable position. Verify canonical height/reach endpoints and mass plausibility separately from natural-looking silhouettes. Inspect joint motion, hair/face combinations and same-country diversity on desktop/mobile in both themes, then save/load, grooming, aging and career continuity with reduced motion. Retain unresolved gaps explicitly; the complete appearance and longitudinal acceptance gates remain open.

Inspect isolated changes and combinations at actual desktop/mobile dossier size in both themes. |

Front and side views must show compact-heavy, tall-heavy and tall-light bodies. Clothing/pads are a separate layer; estimated composition is labeled in provenance. |

Heavy-fast bodies retain mass. Joint motion is coherent, reduced motion works, and grooming/gray changes preserve recognition through trades, aging and save restoration. |

- **Hair:** independently represent natural pigmentation, dye, graying, texture/curvature, strand width, length, density, hairline and cut; support straight, wavy, curly and coily hair, styled braids/locs and shaved/bald states. Brows and facial hair retain their own color, density and grooming controls. Produce a coverage manifest from the renderer's actual supported values.
- **Skin and face:** continuous complexion and undertone variation; stable individual eyes/lids/brows, nose, lips, cheeks, jaw/chin and ears, with restrained asymmetry and optional skin details. Lighting must preserve differences across the complexion range in both themes.
- **Eyes:** preserve the seven existing color categories for compatibility while adding optional versioned shade variation and bounded inner/outer iris pattern controls. Eye color must remain visible with naturally sized eyes at dossier size. Existing saves retain their original appearance; new parameter ranges and frequencies remain artistic unless separately calibrated.
- **Frame and proportions:** canonical height, weight and recorded arm length, wingspan and hand size drive the skeleton and visible physique. Separate skeletal width and torso/leg balance from chest, waist, neck, arm, thigh and calf mass allocation. Two equal-height/weight players may have different plausible frames. Recorded measurables stay authoritative; estimated anatomy and composition stay labeled as estimates.
- **Athleticism:** strength/power can affect bounded muscular definition and movement cues; speed, agility and jumping inform workouts and animation. A strength-only change preserves total mass and height; a speed-only change does not automatically remove mass. Avoid using overall rating or demographic identity as a physique preset.
- **Lifetime and proof:** preserve recognizable face, natural pigmentation and skeletal individuality through trades, team changes, aging and save/load; change grooming and body mass separately. Inspect one-control-at-a-time pairs, all supported appearance values, same-country diversity and common-scale full-body counterfactuals on desktop/mobile in both themes. Include blink/breathing motion and reduced-motion behavior. Record remaining gaps rather than treating stored fields or source tests as rendered proof.

Creation rating contract: normalize both positional bias and archetype strengths against the actual overall formula. Strengths alter a player's skill shape, rather than giving every archetype an overall subsidy. For the standard rostered population (active plus practice), versioned fictional population-mean upper bounds are 2.5% rated 90+ and 1% rated 95+; these are rating-design limits, not measured NFL demographics or deterministic guarantees for every seed. Use a predeclared five-sigma sample-size diagnostic allowance per league and for the pooled ensemble, without claiming formal confidence coverage. The fixed ensemble includes consecutive seeds 1–32 and the four original regression seeds, including independent review failures 2/3. This deliberately replaces the older compressed-generator test's 0.765% ceiling and its zero-elite requirements for several positions. Keep exceptional players possible across all positions, a broad lower tail and wide team spans. Longitudinal elite-density and progression gates remain unchanged and must still pass.

Every generated player must have an individual visible identity, including players with the same country, heritage and position. Required variation includes hairstyle, natural hair color and dye, texture, skin tone and undertone, eye color and shape, facial structure, facial hair, stature, skeletal proportions and regional body volume. A stored attribute that has no visible effect does not satisfy this requirement.

Generation order is country/football upbringing → local, diaspora or mixed family history → overlapping natural appearance priors → individual facial and skeletal variation → personal grooming. Country demographic references inform family-history plausibility; the dated NFL survey informs league representation. Do not assign one race, skin tone, haircut or face to a nationality.

Physique generation must use correlated, position-specific height/mass/limb reference cohorts, preserving overlap and unusual athletes. Canonical measurements determine stature and reach; an individual regional mass allocation determines chest, waist, neck, arms and legs. Regional allocations sum to the declared model budget. Strength/power can affect bounded definition or redistribute that budget; speed alone must not change mass or skeleton. This is an explicit visual model, not a body-fat measurement. Compare equal height/different weight, equal weight/different height, equal measurements/different strength, and equal measurements/different speed at a common camera scale.

Research calibration records must identify source year, sampled population, units, missingness and selection bias; separate observed values from creative coefficients. The user's requirement is individual variation across the whole player, including people with the same birthplace or heritage. NFL reporting categories, family origins, birthplace, upbringing and appearance remain separate. Physical attributes affect the bounded visual physique model through the player's own measurements; heritage does not alter athletic ability or body geometry.

Persist values or retain the exact saved recipe's decoder. A face seed alone is insufficient if changing the generation formula changes an existing player. Check phenotype combinations and visibly distinct hairstyles, finite geometry and measurement/body ordering. Run paired demographic-only changes through ability, development, injury and valuation, proving unchanged football outcomes and simulation RNG. Verify old/new saves and representative portraits in both themes at desktop/mobile widths. Record actual results and unresolved gaps before release.

Matching league totals alone does not pass. |

Add restrained skin details and asymmetry. | A deterministic gallery covers every supported style/color/texture and grooming state. Controlled pairs change one trait at a time and show that change without replacing the face. |

Derive shoulder/chest/waist/neck/arm volume with stable individual variation; use bounded strength/power cues without equating speed with leanness. Keep overlapping position builds. | Same-height/different-weight, same-weight/different-height, same-measurements/different-strength and different-arm-length comparisons. ## Athletic consistency and individual variation

Additional primary-source research is recorded in the research document's “Additional research” section. Implement the following as part of the existing identity and portrait scope:

1. **Country-specific family histories.** Preserve birthplace, upbringing, ancestry and the dated survey reporting category separately. Extend Māori and distinct Pacific heritage vocabularies, diaspora families and multiple heritage choices. Country-conditioned preferences must retain within-country diversity; census percentages cannot stand in for recruitment rates. Export the assumed country conditionals for review before declaring calibration complete.
2. **Shared workout authority.** Replace independently uniform draft workout draws with one versioned physical model. Forty and ten-yard split reflect speed/acceleration; shuttle/cone reflect agility; jumps reflect explosive power; bench reflects strength/endurance with bounded residuals. Share height, mass and limb measurements with scouting and rendering. Record event, units, precision, date and measured/fictional provenance; preserve missing tests. The fictional v1 foundation is implemented and focused-tested, including actual draft-to-roster/save transitions. Its coefficients remain creative priors; reference-cohort calibration and comprehensive browser presentation remain open.
3. **Separate identity, grooming and physique.** Persistent facial geometry and natural pigmentation survive team changes, aging and save/load. Grooming can change without replacing the face. Body changes follow measured height/mass and physical attributes with bounded individual variation. No racial, country or overall-rating body template is allowed. Haircut, texture, natural color, dye, density, hairline, beard and eye color each require visibly effective controls.
4. **Controlled and population evidence.** Add individual-trait portrait pairs for every supported hairstyle, hair color, eye color, texture and facial-hair state; include same-demographic players with varied appearances. Compare matched bodies for mass, stature, strength and limb length at the same camera scale. Validate within-position workout correlations and residual variation without forcing perfect rank ordering. Verify canonical measurements and workout events through save/load and draft-to-roster transitions. Imported facts stay authoritative; absent facts remain absent.

Completion requires rendered proof that each advertised appearance field changes the image, coherent athletic measurements, and documented population results. The existing bust/body renderer and focused checks are foundation evidence; they do not establish completion of this expanded acceptance scope.
